# Issue #52 Help/FAQ 全量审阅结果

## R3 内容重审（Issue #79，2026-08-20）

本节处理旧台账在 `help_inventory.py --review-results` 下的
`coverage_equal=false`。#75 Part B 曾将几乎全部 `current_chinese` 不匹配归为
内容漂移；逐 identity 对照后确认，32/32 个现行 ZH 值都逐字等于 #52 已批准并
落地的最终值（旧卡片的 `proposed_translation`），没有发生新的 ZH 内容漂移。
旧台账仍保存“修改前中文 + 修改建议”，因此在候选落地后与现行值不一致。

### 漂移范围与证据复用

- 29/32 个 identity 的英文源未变；其 identity、现行 EN/ZH、语义结论、生命周期
  和相关术语权威均满足证据复用条件。
- 3 个英文源发生变化：`faq:randart`、`help:macro-menu`、
  `help:wiz-monster`。这些变化正是 #52 候选中同步落地的英文事实/结构修复：
  神器附魔变异例外、宏文件的实际保存位置，以及 wizard 怪物规格中的拼写、
  markup 和法术槽标志说明；现行 ZH 已逐条覆盖修复后的英文事实。
- `docs/glossary.md` 从旧 SHA 变化到当前 SHA 的内容仅涉及 #71 独特怪物称号域，
  不改变 Help/FAQ 使用的术语裁决。
- #81 已将 producer/consumer/evidence locator 改为稳定锚点并先行合并；本次按
  当前工具输出重新绑定，不修改消费者代码。

### 重新冻结的绑定

- 基线：`23956ebfbdc5316cfad5e93a9b0183ad76b3727f`。
- 术语表 SHA-256：
  `366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e`。
- inventory SHA-256：
  `0d410480a84ca431eb259614c9b7d04c0559e0bda0e667589ed6a941e8240f59`。
- 身份仍为 13 个 Help + 19 个 FAQ Q/A pair，共 32 个；blocking violations 为
  0。FAQ 文件顺序仅作 authoring evidence，运行时菜单仍由 DBM 迭代，顺序不受
  文件顺序保证。

### 重审结论

- 32 个 identity 全部为 `keep`，无 `adjust`、`retranslate` 或 defer。
- 不改 `crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt`、
  `crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt`、Help/FAQ key、load order、消费者或
  游戏机制；仅重冻结本台账的机械字段、当前术语绑定与 R3 结论。
- 下方严格证据块按当前 inventory 顺序保存每个 identity 的唯一证据卡和终态
  结论。#52 首次审阅及后续两项术语修正最终记录的 19 个 `adjust` 与 13 个
  `retranslate` 已全部落地，原理由和被否决方案保留在各卡片中作为历史证据。

help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"<h>控制台与 WebTiles 键码</h>\n\n键码可以输入为数字，在某些情况下也可以使用易读的名称。\n下表同时给出数字键码和名称；带修饰键的按键通常必须用数字输入。\n当键码作为宏操作的一部分输入时，应使用以下格式表示键码n：\n<w>\\{n}</w>。在rc文件中以数字定义键码时也应使用这种格式。例如，\n定义由F1触发的宏时，可以写成“\\{F1\\}”或“\\{-265}”。\n宏定义中的命名按键可以省略\\{}。\n\n正数值是ASCII字符码，负数值是Crawl内部的特殊键码。\n负号不可省略，否则无法正确解释这些键码。键盘差异很大，\n因此并非所有键盘都支持以下绑定；下文会特别注明常见差异。\n\n下面列出一些常用绑定按键，供快速查阅。\n\n<w>8</w>: Backspace（和/或Delete，取决于键盘）\n<w>9</w>: Tab\n<w>13</w>: Enter（和/或Return键，取决于键盘）\n<w>27</w>: Esc\n\n<w>-233</w>: Shift-Tab\n<w>-222</w>: Ctrl-Tab（在某些键盘上）\n<w>-255</w>: Delete（在某些键盘上）\n<w>-265</w>: F1   <w>-264</w>: F2   <w>-263</w>: F3   <w>-262</w>: F4   <w>-261</w>: F5\n<w>-260</w>: F6   <w>-259</w>: F7   <w>-258</w>: F8   <w>-257</w>: F9   <w>-256</w>: F10\n\n键码定义到F22，但不一定适用于所有构建。\n（在WebTiles中，F11和F12为保留键。）\n\n<w>数字小键盘与导航键：</w>\n\n在许多键盘上，<w>numlock</w>会在数字键和导航键之间切换。\n这些键的修饰键不一定适用于所有键盘。带修饰键的数字小键盘按键可用时，\n无论numlock状态如何，Shift和Ctrl通常都会使这些按键被视为带修饰的导航键。\n\n导航键：\n\n<w>-245</w>: PgDn    <w>-234</w>: Shift-PgDn    <w>-223</w>: Ctrl-PgDn\n<w>-246</w>: PgUp    <w>-235</w>: Shift-PgUp    <w>-224</w>: Ctrl-PgUp\n<w>-247</w>: Clear   <w>-236</w>: Shift-Clear   <w>-225</w>: Ctrl-Clear\n<w>-248</w>: End     <w>-237</w>: Shift-End     <w>-226</w>: Ctrl-End\n<w>-249</w>: Home    <w>-238</w>: Shift-Home    <w>-227</w>: Ctrl-Home\n<w>-250</w>: Insert  <w>-239</w>: Shift-Insert  <w>-228</w>: Ctrl-Insert\n<w>-251</w>: Right   <w>-240</w>: Shift-Right   <w>-229</w>: Ctrl-Right\n<w>-252</w>: Left    <w>-241</w>: Shift-Left    <w>-230</w>: Ctrl-Left\n<w>-253</w>: Down    <w>-242</w>: Shift-Down    <w>-231</w>: Ctrl-Down\n<w>-254</w>: Up      <w>-243</w>: Shift-Up      <w>-232</w>: Ctrl-Up\n\n数字小键盘：\n\n<w>-1007</w>: NP7   <w>-1008</w>: NP8   <w>-1009</w>: NP9\n<w>-1004</w>: NP4   <w>-1005</w>: NP5   <w>-1006</w>: NP6\n<w>-1001</w>: NP1   <w>-1002</w>: NP2   <w>-1003</w>: NP3\n<w>-1000</w>: NP0\n<w>-1010</w>: NPenter\n<w>-1012</w>: NP/   <w>-1015</w>: NP*   <w>-1016</w>: NP+   <w>-1018</w>: NP-\n<w>-1019</w>: NP.   <w>-1021</w>: NP=\n\n某些数字小键盘标点键可能无法与对应的“常规”按键区分；\n这同样取决于键盘和构建。\n","current_english":"<h>Keycodes for console and webtiles</h>\n\nKeycodes can be entered as numbers, or in some cases, human readable strings.\nThe following tables give both the numeric keycode, and the string; modified\nkeys generally must be entered by number. When entering a keycode  as part of a\nmacro action, it should be wrapped using the following syntax for keycode n:\n<w>\\{n}</w>. This should also be used when defining a keycode by number in your\nrc file. Example: when defining a macro that triggers F1, you can write\n\"\\{F1\\}\" or \"\\{-265}\". For named keys as part of a macro definition, the \\{}\nmay be omitted.\n\nPositive numeric values are ASCII character codes. Negative values are\ncrawl-internal keycodes for special keys. The sign is required for these to\nbe interpreted correctly. Keyboards vary quite a bit, and so not all of these\nbindings are available on all keyboards; cases where there is often variation\nare indicated specifically below.\n\nThe following is a quick-reference for some of the main keys you might want to\nbind.\n\n<w>8</w>: Backspace (and/or Delete, depending on keyboard)\n<w>9</w>: Tab\n<w>13</w>: Enter (and/or the Return key, depending on keyboard)\n<w>27</w>: Esc\n\n<w>-233</w>: Shift-Tab\n<w>-222</w>: Ctrl-Tab (on some keyboards)\n<w>-255</w>: Delete (on some keyboards)\n<w>-265</w>: F1   <w>-264</w>: F2   <w>-263</w>: F3   <w>-262</w>: F4   <w>-261</w>: F5\n<w>-260</w>: F6   <w>-259</w>: F7   <w>-258</w>: F8   <w>-257</w>: F9   <w>-256</w>: F10\n\nKeycodes up to F22 are defined, but may not work on all builds. (On webtiles,\nF11 and F12 are reserved.)\n\n<w>Number pad and navigation keys:</w>\n\nOn many keyboards, <w>numlock</w> switches between the number keys and\nnavigation keys. Modifiers for these keys may not work on all keyboards. When\nmodified number pad keys do work, Shift and Ctrl typically treats those keys as\nmodified navigation keys regardless of numlock status.\n\nNavigation keys:\n\n<w>-245</w>: PgDn    <w>-234</w>: Shift-PgDn    <w>-223</w>: Ctrl-PgDn\n<w>-246</w>: PgUp    <w>-235</w>: Shift-PgUp    <w>-224</w>: Ctrl-PgUp\n<w>-247</w>: Clear   <w>-236</w>: Shift-Clear   <w>-225</w>: Ctrl-Clear\n<w>-248</w>: End     <w>-237</w>: Shift-End     <w>-226</w>: Ctrl-End\n<w>-249</w>: Home    <w>-238</w>: Shift-Home    <w>-227</w>: Ctrl-Home\n<w>-250</w>: Insert  <w>-239</w>: Shift-Insert  <w>-228</w>: Ctrl-Insert\n<w>-251</w>: Right   <w>-240</w>: Shift-Right   <w>-229</w>: Ctrl-Right\n<w>-252</w>: Left    <w>-241</w>: Shift-Left    <w>-230</w>: Ctrl-Left\n<w>-253</w>: Down    <w>-242</w>: Shift-Down    <w>-231</w>: Ctrl-Down\n<w>-254</w>: Up      <w>-243</w>: Shift-Up      <w>-232</w>: Ctrl-Up\n\nNumber pad keys:\n\n<w>-1007</w>: NP7   <w>-1008</w>: NP8   <w>-1009</w>: NP9\n<w>-1004</w>: NP4   <w>-1005</w>: NP5   <w>-1006</w>: NP6\n<w>-1001</w>: NP1   <w>-1002</w>: NP2   <w>-1003</w>: NP3\n<w>-1000</w>: NP0\n<w>-1010</w>: NPenter\n<w>-1012</w>: NP/   <w>-1015</w>: NP*   <w>-1016</w>: NP+   <w>-1018</w>: NP-\n<w>-1019</w>: NP.   <w>-1021</w>: NP=\n\nSome number pad punctuation keys may not be distinguished from their \"regular\"\ncounterparts, again depending on help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"dependency_group":"macros, platform keys, and wizard","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","english":"<h>Keycodes for console and webtiles</h>\n\nKeycodes can be entered as numbers, or in some cases, human readable strings.\nThe following tables give both the numeric keycode, and the string; modified\nkeys generally must be entered by number. When entering a keycode  as part of a\nmacro action, it should be wrapped using the following syntax for keycode n:\n<w>\\{n}</w>. This should also be used when defining a keycode by number in your\nrc file. Example: when defining a macro that triggers F1, you can write\n\"\\{F1\\}\" or \"\\{-265}\". For named keys as part of a macro definition, the \\{}\nmay be omitted.\n\nPositive numeric values are ASCII character codes. Negative values are\ncrawl-internal keycodes for special keys. The sign is required for these to\nbe interpreted correctly. Keyboards vary quite a bit, and so not all of these\nbindings are available on all keyboards; cases where there is often variation\nare indicated specifically below.\n\nThe following is a quick-reference for some of the main keys you might want to\nbind.\n\n<w>8</w>: Backspace (and/or Delete, depending on keyboard)\n<w>9</w>: Tab\n<w>13</w>: Enter (and/or the Return key, depending on keyboard)\n<w>27</w>: Esc\n\n<w>-233</w>: Shift-Tab\n<w>-222</w>: Ctrl-Tab (on some keyboards)\n<w>-255</w>: Delete (on some keyboards)\n<w>-265</w>: F1   <w>-264</w>: F2   <w>-263</w>: F3   <w>-262</w>: F4   <w>-261</w>: F5\n<w>-260</w>: F6   <w>-259</w>: F7   <w>-258</w>: F8   <w>-257</w>: F9   <w>-256</w>: F10\n\nKeycodes up to F22 are defined, but may not work on all builds. (On webtiles,\nF11 and F12 are reserved.)\n\n<w>Number pad and navigation keys:</w>\n\nOn many keyboards, <w>numlock</w> switches between the number keys and\nnavigation keys. Modifiers for these keys may not work on all keyboards. When\nmodified number pad keys do work, Shift and Ctrl typically treats those keys as\nmodified navigation keys regardless of numlock status.\n\nNavigation keys:\n\n<w>-245</w>: PgDn    <w>-234</w>: Shift-PgDn    <w>-223</w>: Ctrl-PgDn\n<w>-246</w>: PgUp    <w>-235</w>: Shift-PgUp    <w>-224</w>: Ctrl-PgUp\n<w>-247</w>: Clear   <w>-236</w>: Shift-Clear   <w>-225</w>: Ctrl-Clear\n<w>-248</w>: End     <w>-237</w>: Shift-End     <w>-226</w>: Ctrl-End\n<w>-249</w>: Home    <w>-238</w>: Shift-Home    <w>-227</w>: Ctrl-Home\n<w>-250</w>: Insert  <w>-239</w>: Shift-Insert  <w>-228</w>: Ctrl-Insert\n<w>-251</w>: Right   <w>-240</w>: Shift-Right   <w>-229</w>: Ctrl-Right\n<w>-252</w>: Left    <w>-241</w>: Shift-Left    <w>-230</w>: Ctrl-Left\n<w>-253</w>: Down    <w>-242</w>: Shift-Down    <w>-231</w>: Ctrl-Down\n<w>-254</w>: Up      <w>-243</w>: Shift-Up      <w>-232</w>: Ctrl-Up\n\nNumber pad keys:\n\n<w>-1007</w>: NP7   <w>-1008</w>: NP8   <w>-1009</w>: NP9\n<w>-1004</w>: NP4   <w>-1005</w>: NP5   <w>-1006</w>: NP6\n<w>-1001</w>: NP1   <w>-1002</w>: NP2   <w>-1003</w>: NP3\n<w>-1000</w>: NP0\n<w>-1010</w>: NPenter\n<w>-1012</w>: NP/   <w>-1015</w>: NP*   <w>-1016</w>: NP+   <w>-1018</w>: NP-\n<w>-1019</w>: NP.   <w>-1021</w>: NP=\n\nSome number pad punctuation keys may not be distinguished from their \"regular\"\ncounterparts, again depending on keyboard and build.\n","english_key_line":667,"english_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</h>":1,"</w>":67,"<h>":1,"<w>":67},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":["265","8","9","13","27","233","222","255","265","264","263","262","261","260","259","258","257","256","245","234","223","246","235","224","247","236","225","248","237","226","249","238","227","250","239","228","251","240","229","252","241","230","253","242","231","254","243","232","1007","1008","1009","1004","1005","1006","1001","1002","1003","1000","1010","1012","1015","1016","1018","1019","1021"],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":["and/","and/","NP/"],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["\\{n}","8","9","13","27","-233","-222","-255","-265","-264","-263","-262","-261","-260","-259","-258","-257","-256","Number help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"<h>跨层移动（选择分支）：\n 按下分支对应的快捷字母，选择要前往的分支。\n\n 选择分支后，系统会提示你输入要前往该分支的哪一层\n （届时可查看更详细的帮助）。\n\n <w>Tab</w>    ：重复上一次跨层移动。\n <w>.</w>      ：前往当前分支中的某一层。\n <w><<</w>     ：经由最近的上行楼梯上楼。\n <w>></w>      ：经由最近的下行楼梯下楼。\n <w>Ctrl-P</w> ：前往当前分支的上级分支中的某一层。\n <w>*</w>      ：显示可用导航点（若已设置）。\n <w>0</w>-<w>9</w>    ：前往相应编号的导航点。\n <w>_</w>      ：显示可用祭坛（若已发现）。","current_english":"<h>Interlevel Travel (choose a branch):\n Use the shortcut letter for a branch to select the branch for travel.\n\n Once you select a branch, you will be prompted for a depth in that\n branch (more help is available there).\n\n <w>Tab</w>    : Repeat last interlevel travel.\n <w>.</w>      : Travel to a level in the current branch.\n <w><<</w>      : Go up the nearest stairs.\n <w>></w>      : Go down the nearest stairs.\n <w>Ctrl-P</w> : Travel to a level in the branch above this one.\n <w>*</w>      : Show available waypoints (if any are set).\n help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"<h>跨层移动（移动到所选分支的某一楼层）\n 键入要前往的楼层数，然后按Enter键，或使用：\n <w>Enter</w>  ：移动到默认楼层。\n <w>0</w>      ：移动到当前分支的入口。\n <w><<</w> entrance to the current branch.\n <w><<</w>      : Change the default to one level above the current.\n <w>></w>      : Change default to one level below the current.\n <w>-</w> or <w>p</w> : Change default to the branch above this one.\n <w>$</w>      : Change default to deepest visited level in this branch.\n <w>^</w>      : Change default to the entrance to the current {"actual_behavior":"已知物品菜单可逐项或按类别覆盖自动拾取默认值，并在已知/未知页面切换、恢复默认或用正则批量选择。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"                   <h>已知物品菜单</h>\n\n<h>定位和选择物品</h>\n使用通常的定位键（<w>方向</w>键、<w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>、<w>home</w>、<w>end</w>）选择物品，\n使用<w>空格</w>/<w>.</w>切换所选物品的自动拾取状态。\n按下某类物品对应的字母键，也会切换该类物品的自动拾取状态。\n\n<h>选择物品种类</h>\n以灰色显示的物品类型会采用配置文件中设定的默认自动拾取选项。\n（对你的角色无用的物品会以<darkgrey>深灰色</darkgrey>显示。）\n选定一类物品后会覆盖这些选项（以<w>白色</w>文本显示），切换物品为以下状态：\n <w>+</w>  将自动拾取此类物品。\n <w>-</w>  将不再自动拾取此类物品。\n还可以切换某一类别中所有未知物品的自动拾取状态。\n\n<h>各类物品的快捷键</h>\n你可以按以下键快速选择某类物品：\n<w>!</w>   药水      <w>?</w>   卷轴      <w>(</w>   弹药        <w>\"</w>   项链\n<w>=</w>   戒指      <w>/</w>   魔杖      <w>|</w>   法杖\n全局选择：<w>*</w>   反选   <w>^</w>   全部重置为默认状态\n\n<h>查看未知和已知物品</h>\n按<w>\\</w>和<w>-</w>切换已知和未知物品菜单。\n请注意，未知物品菜单中没有上述功能。\n\n<h>将自动拾取重置为默认设定</h>\n按<w>Ctrl-D</w>，再按物品字母或类别快捷键，会取消覆盖设置，\n并将所选的一件或多件物品恢复为默认自动拾取设置。\n\n<h>正则表达式</h>\n你可以按<w>Ctrl-F</w>输入一个单词（或一个正则表达式）。\n所匹配的物品都会被选定。\n","current_english":"                   <h>Known items menu</h>\n\n<h>Navigation and selecting items</h>\nUse the usual navigation keys (<w>arrow</w> keys, <w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>, <w>home</w>, <w>end</w>) to select\nitems, and <w>Space</w>/<w>.</w> to toggle the autopickup mode for the selected item type.\nPressing an item type's letter key will also toggle its autopickup state.\n\n<h>Selecting item types</h>\nItem types displayed in grey use the default autopickup options set in your\nconfiguration file. (Items that are useless to your character are displayed\nin <darkgrey>dark grey</darkgrey>.) Selecting a type of item overrides those options\n(indicated by <w>white</w> text), and toggles the item between the states:\n <w>+</w>  Items of this type will be picked up automatically.\n <w>-</w>  Items of this type will not be picked up automatically.\nAutopickup can also be toggled for all unknown items of a given category.\n\n<h>Category shortcuts</h>\nYou can quickly select all item types in a given category by pressing:\n<w>!</w>   potions      <w>?</w>   scrolls      <w>(</w>   ammunition        <w>\"</w>   amulets\n<w>=</w>   rings        <w>/</w>   wands        <w>|</w>   magical staves\nGlobal selects:  <w>*</w>   invert all   <w>^</w>   reset all to default\n\n<h>Viewing unknown and known items</h>\n<w>\\</w> and <w>-</w> toggle between the known and unknown items menus. Note\nthat none of the above features are available from the unknown items menu.\n\n<h>Restoring autopickup defaults</h>\nPressing <w>Ctrl-D</w> followed by an item letter or a category shortcut\ncancels the override and restores the selected item or items to their default\nsettings.\n\n<h>Regular expressions</h>\nYou can press <w>Ctrl-F</w> to enter a word (or a regular expression).\nAll matching items will be selected.\n","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"items, pickup, and known-items menus","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/known-items.cc@\"known-menu\"#1","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/help.txt@known-menu","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt@known-menu","crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"],"fact_sha256":"c1cde4bfdb824d28f509eb184b089a6ad0bd5944fe56f8a6bacb6ad3a2356caf","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"help:known-menu","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"anchor":"crawl-ref/source/known-items.cc@\"known-menu\"#1","guards":[],"path":"crawl-ref/source/known-items.cc"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"                   <h>已知物品菜单</h>\n\n<h>定位和选择物品</h>\n使用通常的定位键（<w>方向</w>键、<w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>、<w>home</w>、<w>end</w>）选择物品，\n使用<w>空格</w>/<w>.</w>切换所选物品的自动拾取状态。\n按下某类物品对应的字母键，也会切换该类物品的自动拾取状态。\n\n<h>选择物品种类</h>\n以灰色显示的物品类型会采用配置文件中设定的默认自动拾取选项。\n（对你的角色无用的物品会以<darkgrey>深灰色</darkgrey>显示。）\n选定一类物品后会覆盖这些选项（以<w>白色</w>文本显示），切换物品为以下状态：\n <w>+</w>  将自动拾取此类物品。\n <w>-</w>  将不再自动拾取此类物品。\n还可以切换某一类别中所有未知物品的自动拾取状态。\n\n<h>各类物品的快捷键</h>\n你可以按以下键快速选择某类物品：\n<w>!</w>   药水      <w>?</w>   卷轴      <w>(</w>   弹药        <w>\"</w>   项链\n<w>=</w>   戒指      <w>/</w>   魔杖      <w>|</w>   法杖\n全局选择：<w>*</w>   反选   <w>^</w>   全部重置为默认状态\n\n<h>查看未知和已知物品</h>\n按<w>\\</w>和<w>-</w>切换已知和未知物品菜单。\n请注意，未知物品菜单中没有上述功能。\n\n<h>将自动拾取重置为默认设定</h>\n按<w>Ctrl-D</w>，再按物品字母或类别快捷键，会取消覆盖设置，\n并将所选的一件或多件物品恢复为默认自动拾取设置。\n\n<h>正则表达式</h>\n你可以按<w>Ctrl-F</w>输入一个单词（或一个正则表达式）。\n所匹配的物品都会被选定。\n","chinese_key_line":29,"chinese_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</darkgrey>":1,"</h>":7,"</w>":23,"<darkgrey>":1,"<h>":7,"<w>":23},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<darkgrey>","</darkgrey>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["方向","pgdn","pgup","home","end","空格",".","白色","+","-","!","?","(","\"","=","/","|","*","^","\\","-","Ctrl-D","Ctrl-F"]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"dependency_group":"items, pickup, and known-items menus","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","english":"                   <h>Known items menu</h>\n\n<h>Navigation and selecting items</h>\nUse the usual navigation keys (<w>arrow</w> keys, <w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>, <w>home</w>, <w>end</w>) to select\nitems, and <w>Space</w>/<w>.</w> to toggle the autopickup mode for the selected item type.\nPressing an item type's letter key will also toggle its autopickup state.\n\n<h>Selecting item types</h>\nItem types displayed in grey use the default autopickup options set in your\nconfiguration file. (Items that are useless to your character are displayed\nin <darkgrey>dark grey</darkgrey>.) Selecting a type of item overrides those options\n(indicated by <w>white</w> text), and toggles the item between the states:\n <w>+</w>  Items of this type will be picked up automatically.\n <w>-</w>  Items of this type will not be picked up automatically.\nAutopickup can also be toggled for all unknown items of a given category.\n\n<h>Category shortcuts</h>\nYou can quickly select all item types in a given category by pressing:\n<w>!</w>   potions      <w>?</w>   scrolls      <w>(</w>   ammunition        <w>\"</w>   amulets\n<w>=</w>   rings        <w>/</w>   wands        <w>|</w>   magical staves\nGlobal selects:  <w>*</w>   invert all   <w>^</w>   reset all to default\n\n<h>Viewing unknown and known items</h>\n<w>\\</w> and <w>-</w> toggle between the known and unknown items menus. Note\nthat none of the above features are available from the unknown items menu.\n\n<h>Restoring autopickup defaults</h>\nPressing <w>Ctrl-D</w> followed by an item letter or a category shortcut\ncancels the override and restores the selected item or items to their default\nsettings.\n\n<h>Regular expressions</h>\nYou can press <w>Ctrl-F</w> to enter a word (or a regular expression).\nAll matching items will be selected.\n","english_key_line":31,"english_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</darkgrey>":1,"</h>":7,"</w>":23,"<darkgrey>":1,"<h>":7,"<w>":23},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<darkgrey>","</darkgrey>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["arrow","pgdn","pgup","home","end","Space",".","white","+","-","!","?","(","\"","=","/","|","*","^","\\","-","Ctrl-D","Ctrl-F"]},"fact_sha256":"c1cde4bfdb824d28f509eb184b089a6ad0bd5944fe56f8a6bacb6ad3a2356caf","identity":"help:known-menu","key":"known-menu","kind":"help","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"anchor":"crawl-ref/source/known-items.cc@\"known-menu\"#1","guards":[],"path":"crawl-ref/source/known-items.cc"}],"structural_differences":["w_contents"],"token_contract_equal":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":false}},"proposed_translation":null,"reentry_trigger":"对应现行 help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"<h>楼层地图（主界面按“<w>X</w>”）：</h>\n<w>Esc</w>        ：离开楼层地图（也可按<w>Space</w>或任意未使用的键）。\n<w>Dir.</w>       ：移动光标。\n<w>Shift-Dir.</w> ：大步移动光标（默认每次 7 格）。\n<w>-</w>或<w>+</w>     ：将楼层地图向上或向下滚动 20 格。\n<w>.</w>          ：开始自动旅行（也可按<w>Enter</w>、<w>,</w>或<w>;</w>）。\n             （如果光标仍在 @ 处，则将其移到上一次旅行的目的地。）\n<w>[</w>或<w>]</w>     ：查看相邻的上一层或下一层。\n             （如果当前高亮的是已知楼梯，则将光标移到该楼梯通往的位置。）\n<w>G</w>          ：选择分支和层数，查看另一个楼层。\n<w>o</w>          ：将光标移动到下一个自动探索目标。\n<w>{</w>或<w>}</w>     ：缩小或放大地图（仅限贴图版）。\n\n<h>旅行禁区</h>\n<w>e</w>          ：创建旅行禁区、调整其半径或将其移除。\n<w>R#</w>         ：创建指定半径的旅行禁区，或将其半径改为 #；\n                    # 为 1 到 8 的数字。\n<w>E</w>          ：循环查看旅行禁区。\n<w>Ctrl-E</w>     ：清除本层的所有旅行禁区。\n\n<h>导航点</h>\n<w>w</w>          ：在当前位置设置导航点。\n<w>W</w>          ：循环查看本层的所有导航点。\n\n<h>循环查看地图要素</h>\n<w><<</w>或<w>></w>     ：循环查看上行或下行楼梯。\n<w>^</w>          ：循环查看陷阱。\n<w>\\</w>或<w>Tab</w>   ：循环查看商店和传送门。\n<w>I</w>          ：正向循环查看所有物品。\n<w>O</w>          ：反向循环查看所有物品。\n<w>_</w>          ：循环查看祭坛。\n\n<h>其他</h>\n<w>v</w>          ：查看所指地图格最后一次记忆的状态。\n<w>!</w>          ：注释楼层。\n<w>Ctrl-C</w>     ：清除怪物记忆。\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>     ：遗忘楼层地图。\n<w>Ctrl-U</w>     ：恢复已遗忘的楼层地图。{{\nif you.wizard() then\n    return \"\\n\\n\"\n          .. \"<h>巫师模式命令</h>\\n\"\n          .. \"<w>T</w>          ：传送到光标位置。\\n\"\n          .. \"<w>Ctrl-X</w>     ：彻底清空地图。\"\nend }}","current_english":"<h>Level Map ('<w>X</w>' in the main screen):</h>\n<w>Esc</w>        : Leave level map (also <w>Space</w> or any unused key).\n<w>Dir.</w>       : Move the cursor.\n<w>Shift-Dir.</w> : Move the cursor in larger steps (7 by default).\n<w>-</w> or <w>+</w>     : Scroll level map 20 grids up or down.\n<w>.</w>          : Start travel (also <w>Enter</w> and <w>,</w> and <w>;</w>).\n             (Moves cursor to the last travel destination if still on @.)\n<w>[</w> or <w>]</w>     : Examine the next higher or lower level.\n             (Moves cursor to the staircase destination if a known staircase is\n             highlighted.)\n<w>G</w>          : View another level by choosing a branch and depth.\n<w>o</w>          : Move the cursor to the next autoexplore target.\n<w>{</w> or <w>}</w>     : Zoom out or in (tiles only).\n\n<h>Travel exclusions</h>\n<w>e</w>          : Create a travel exclusion, change its radius, or remove it.\n<w>R#</w>         : Create a travel exclusion with a specified radius or change\n             its radius to #, where # is a number from 1 to 8.\n<w>E</w>          : Cycle through travel exclusions.\n<w>Ctrl-E</w>     : Erase all travel exclusions on the level.\n\n<h>Waypoints</h>\n<w>w</w>          : Set waypoint to current position.\n<w>W</w>          : Cycle through all waypoints on the level.\n\n<h>Cycle through features</h>\n<w><<</w> or <w>></w>     : Cycle through up or down stairs.\n<w>^</w>          : Cycle through traps.\n<w>\\</w> or <w>Tab</w>   : Cycle through shops and portals.\n<w>I</w>          : Cycle forward through all items.\n<w>O</w>          : Cycle backward through all items.\n<w>_</w>          : Cycle through altars.\n\n<h>Other</h>\n<w>v</w>          : Examine the last remembered state of the indicated tile.\n<w>!</w>          : Annotate level.\n<w>Ctrl-C</w>     : Clear monster memory.\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>     : Forget level map.\n<w>Ctrl-U</w>     : Restore forgotten level map.{{\nif you.wizard() then\n    return \"\\n\\n\"\n          .. \"<h>Wizard commands</h>\\n\"\n          .. \"<w>T</w>          : Teleport to cursor.\\n\"\n          .. \"<w>Ctrl-X</w>     : Completely wipe out map.\"\nend }}","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"map, search, travel, and annotation","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic 权威发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["仅作零散词语替换：跨段落的事实、列表或格式问题无法得到完整修复。","原样保留基线译文：已确认的语义或结构缺陷会继续展示给玩家。"],"reviewer_rationale":"keep（R3 复审，Refs #79）：现行英文源未变；#52 批准的 proposed_translation 已逐字落地为当前中文，且后续 glossary 变化仅涉及不相关的独特怪物称号域。当前 identity、生命周期、生产行为和结构证据已按稳定 locator 重新核对，无需进一步改动。原 retranslate 理由：基线对多项按键行为和条件化说明不自然或失真；完整重译并保持 Lua 比较字符串与控制骨架更可靠。","terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"actual_behavior":"宏菜单区分 keymap、macro 与 action；编辑结果保存到 Crawl 配置的宏文件，名称和位置依平台/构建，多数本地构建为 macro_dir 下的 macro.txt。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"<h>编辑宏和键映射</h>\n\n要编辑现有宏或键映射，请用方向键或热键在菜单中选择。\n（用来浏览菜单的热键项也可通过方向键选中。）要通过输入按键创建新映射，\n请按[<w>~</w>]或选择相应菜单项，再输入该键。\n这样也能创建（或编辑）使用菜单按键的映射。\n按[<w>!</w>]可切换到键映射编辑模式（键映射的详细说明见下文）。\n\n<w>Macros</w>可重新映射输入命令时发送给游戏的按键。\n宏可以重新定义命令键，也可以定义未使用的键。\n宏将一个按键映射为一串按键，而且这些按键会继续传入子菜单。\n\n示例 1：将<w>e</w>映射为<w>p</w>后，按“e”键会触发与“p”键相同的命令\n（默认设置中是自动射击）。\n\n示例 2：将<w>1</w>映射为按键序列<w>zaf</w>后，\n按“1”键会施放字母“a”对应的法术，并使用默认瞄准。\n\n示例 3：将<w>F1</w>映射为<w>Q&a</w>后，\n按F1键会把字母“a”对应的法术设为待发。\n\n<w>Keymaps</w>是更底层的按键替换，会把一个按键完全替换为一串按键。\n建议用键映射统一修改按键，以适配不同的键盘布局。\n键映射取决于情境；未启用特殊情境时，各处都使用默认的“regular”情境。\n系统先处理键映射，宏以键映射的结果为输入。\n\n例如，在某些欧洲键盘布局中，§符号取代了~。因此，\n在常规和菜单键映射情境中都把§映射为~会很有用，\n这样会更容易打开主菜单和宏菜单。（在其他布局中，对应的符号是½。）\n\n用键映射映射命令可能产生意外副作用。例如，不建议通过键映射\n将<w>1</w>映射为<w>zaf</w>，因为输入技能训练目标时会很难键入“1”。\n\n<w>Keycodes</w>：宏和键映射在描述操作时都接受键码，\n输入格式为<w>\\{n}</w>，表示键码<w>n</w>。（使用<w>\\\\</w>输入反斜杠。）\n例如，使用<w>\\{9}</w>输入<w>tab</w>键的键码。\n在宏编辑菜单中使用<w>~~</w>，可以按键码绑定宏和键映射。\n\n<w>Saving</w>：在此菜单编辑的绑定会写入 Crawl 配置的宏文件；\n文件名和位置取决于平台与构建。\n多数本地构建中，该文件是 macro_dir 下的 macro.txt。\noptions 文件中的设置会覆盖这些绑定。\n有关详情，请参阅 options_guide.txt 中的 macro 命令。\n","current_english":"<h>Editing macros and keymaps</h>\n\nTo edit a current macro/keymap, select from the menu using the arrow keys or\nby hotkey. (Hotkeys that are used to navigate the menu can be selected by\narrow keys). To create a new mapping by entering a key, use the [<w>~</w>] key\nor the corresponding menu item, and then enter the key. This allows you to\ncreate (and edit) mappings that use menu keys. Use the [<w>!</w>] key to switch\nto keymap editing mode (see below for more information on keymaps).\n\n<w>Macros</w> allow remapping keys that are sent to the game when inputting\ncommands. This can be used to redefine a command key, or to define an unused\nkey. They map a key to a sequence of keystrokes, and keystrokes do carry over\ninto submenus.\n\nExample 1: mapping <w>e</w> to <w>p</w> would let the `e` key trigger the same\ncommand as the `p` key (which on the default settings is autofire).\n\nExample 2: mapping <w>1</w> to the sequence <w>zaf</w> would let the `1` key\ncast a spell that is on the letter `a` with default targeting.\n\nExample 3: mapping <w>F1</w> to <w>Q&a</w> would let the F1 key quiver the spell\nthat is on letter `a`.\n\n<w>Keymaps</w> are lower-level key substitutions that replace keys entirely with\na sequence of keystrokes. They are recommended for changing keys uniformly to\ndeal with alternative keyboard layouts. Keymaps are contextual, and the\ndefault/\"regular\" context is used anywhere that a non-specialised context is not\nactive. Macros take the result of keymaps as input.\n\nExample: In some European keyboard layouts, the § symbol replaces ~. Therefore,\nit could be useful to keymap § to ~ in both the regular and menu keymap contexts\nso that the main menu and macro menu become a bit easier to access. (In other\nlayouts, this applies instead to ½.)\n\nWhen used for mapping commands, keymaps can have unexpected side effects: e.g.\nmapping <w>1</w> to <w>zaf</w> via keymap is not recommended, because this will\nmake it hard to type `1` when entering a skill target.\n\n<w>Keycodes</w>: Both macros and keymaps accept keycodes when describing the\naction, in the form of <w>\\{n}</w> to enter keycode <w>n</w>. (Use <w>\\\\</w> to\nenter a backslash.) For example, use <w>\\{9}</w> to enter the keycode for the\n<w>tab</w> button. You can bind macros and keymaps by keycode by using <w>~~</w>\nat the macro edit menu.\n\n<w>Saving</w>: Bindings edited at this menu are saved in Crawl's configured\nmacro file. Its name and location depend on the platform and build; in most\nlocal builds it is macro.txt in macro_dir. They will be overridden by settings\nin your options file. See the `macro` command in options_guide.txt for more\ninformation.\n","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"macros, platform keys, and wizard","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n 候选中已经审阅并同步落地的英文事实/结构修复；现行中文完整覆盖修复后的英文内容。当前 identity、生命周期、生产行为和结构证据已按稳定 locator 重新核对，无需进一步改动。原 retranslate 理由：基线对绑定类型和保存位置的说明不够准确；源事实同时更新后，整段重译能保持覆盖优先级和配置说明一致。","terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"actual_behavior":"拾取菜单支持选择、反选、查看、过滤、全部操作以及容器/负重相关显示，具体按键由现有菜单消费者解释。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"                   <h>拾取和丢弃物品</h>\n\n<h>定位和选择物品</h>\n使用通常的定位键（<w>方向</w>键，<w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>、<w>home</w>、<w>end</w>）选择物品，\n使用<w>空格</w>/<w>.</w>切换所选物品的拾取或丢弃状态。\n按下物品的字母键也将切换其拾取/丢弃状态。\n装备、药水、卷轴和可激活物品分别位于不同页面，可用<w>左方向键</w>和<w>右方向键</w>切换。\n按<w>回车</w>确认当前选择的物品。\n\n在按字母选择物品之前输入数字，可以指定要操作的那堆物品的数量。\n\n<h>正则表达式</h>\n你可以按<w>Ctrl-F</w>输入一个单词（或一个正则表达式）。\n所匹配的物品都会被选定。\n\n<h>各类物品的快捷键</h>\n你可以按以下键快速选择某类物品：\n<w>!</w>   药水      <w>(</w>   武器        <w>)</w>   弹药      <w>[</w>   护甲\n<w>/</w>   魔杖      <w>|</w>   法杖        <w>?</w>   卷轴      <w>:</w>   书\n<w>\"</w>   项链      <w>=</w>   戒指        <w>}</w>   杂项\n全局选择：<w>,</w>   全选     <w>*</w>   反选      <w>-</w>   取消全选\n          <w>;</w>   选择最后一个未装备的\n请注意，在丢弃物品时，<w>,</w>命令会应用<w>drop_filter</w>选项，\n缩小可选择或取消选择的物品范围。默认范围是所有无用物品。","current_english":"                   <h>Picking up and dropping items</h>\n\n<h>Navigation and selecting items</h>\nUse the usual navigation keys (<w>arrow</w> keys, <w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>, <w>home</w>, <w>end</w>) to select\nitems, and <w>Space</w>/<w>.</w> to toggle whether the selected item will be picked up or\ndropped. Pressing an item's letter key will also toggle its pickup/drop state.\nGear, potions, scrolls, and evocable items are on separate pages which can be\nswitched between by <w>left arrow</w> and <w>right arrow</w>.\nPress <w>Return</w> to accept the current set of items.\n\nEntering a number before selecting by letter will toggle just that quantity of\na stack of items.\n\n<h>Regular expressions</h>\nYou can press <w>Ctrl-F</w> to enter a word (or a regular expression). All matching\nitems will be selected.\n\n<h>Category shortcuts</h>\nYou can quickly select items by type by pressing:\n<w>!</w>   potions      <w>(</w>   weapons        <w>)</w>   ammunition      <w>[</w>   armour\n<w>/</w>   wands        <w>|</w>   magical staves <w>?</w>   scrolls         <w>:</w>   books\n<w>\"</w>   amulets      <w>=</w>   rings          <w>}</w>   miscellaneous\nGlobal selects:  <w>,</w>   select all     <w>*</w>   invert all      <w>-</w>   deselect all\n                 <w>;</w>   select last unequipped\nNote that for dropping, the <w>,</w> command uses the <w>drop_filter</w> option, which\nnarrows the range of items to be (de)selected. The default is all useless items.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"items, pickup, and known-items menus","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help 选择最后一个未装备的\n请注意，在丢弃物品时，<w>,</w>命令会应用<w>drop_filter</w>选项，\n缩小可选择或取消选择的物品范围。默认范围是所有无用物品。","chinese_key_line":2,"chinese_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</h>":4,"</w>":28,"<h>":4,"<w>":28},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["方向","pgdn","pgup","home","end","空格",".","左方向键","右方向键","回车","Ctrl-F","!","(",")","[","/","|","?",":","\"","=","}",",","*","-",";",",","drop_filter"]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for glossary 变化仅涉及不相关的独特怪物称号域。当前 identity、生命周期、生产行为和结构证据已按稳定 locator 重新核对，无需进一步改动。原 adjust 理由：基线存在局部动作和对象范围不清；按实际菜单行为调整措辞即可，无需改变结构。","terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"actual_behavior":"技能菜单可启停训练、设置目标并切换显示训练百分比、技能成本或目标；目标达成后训练会自动停用。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"                        <h>技能管理</h>\n\n你可以通过杀死怪物（及很少的其他一些活动）获得经验值。\n经验值被用来训练技能。\n在技能界面，你可以选择技能进行训练。\n\n通过按下字母，你可以循环调整是否及如何训练技能：\n  <darkgrey>- 取消选择</darkgrey>  不会训练此技能。\n  + 选择      会训练此技能\n  <w>* 聚焦</w>      此技能在训练时会有更高权重。\n\n按下大写字母可选择此技能并取消选择其他所有技能。\n当你只想训练一种技能时，这个快捷键非常有用。\n\n训练可在两种模式下进行，通过<w>/</w>键切换。\n  - 自动：    技能权重取决于你对它们的使用。\n  - 手动：    经验会被均匀分配给所有激活的技能。\n\n在训练目标模式下，你可以设置技能的训练目标：\n达到目标等级后，系统会自动停用该技能的训练。\n\n训练技能的速度取决于以下因素：\n  - 天赋：在Apt栏中以<white>白色</white>显示。\n数值越高则意味着升级某一技能所需的经验越少。这取决于种族。\n  - 技能手册：这种罕见的书能为一个技能提供<lightred>+4</lightred>的天赋加成。\n\n除此之外，还有一些效果能直接调整技能，\n例如训练了相关技能（交叉训练）或某些神力。\n削弱的技能以<magenta>洋红色</magenta>显示，\n增强的技能以<green>绿色</green>显示。\n训练百分比（以<brown>棕色</brown>显示）表示用于该技能的经验值占比。\n\n除了（取消）选择技能外，还可以进行以下切换：\n  <w>/</w> ：在自动和手动训练模式之间进行切换。\n  <w>*</w> ：显示所有技能，即使是那些目前无用的技能。\n  <w>?</w> ：阅读技能描述。\n  <w>_</w> ：显示或隐藏技能等级的调整（神力、交叉训练）。\n  <w>!</w> ：切换显示训练百分比、技能成本或训练目标。","current_english":"                        <h>Skill management</h>\n\nYou gain experience for killing monsters (and very few other activities). This\nexperience is used to train skills. The skill screen allows you to select which\nskills to train.\n\nBy pressing its letter, you can cycle if and how a skill is trained:\n  <darkgrey>- deselected</darkgrey>    Skill will not be trained.\n  + selected      Skill will be trained.\n  <w>* focused</w>       Skill will be weighted higher for training.\n\nPressing a capital letter selects that skill and deselects all the others.\nThis shortcut is useful when you want to train only one skill.\n\nTraining can be done in two modes, which are toggled with the <w>/</w> key.\n  - automatic:    Skills are weighted based on your use of them.\n  - manual:       Experience is spread evenly across all activated skills.\n\nIn training target mode you can set skill training targets: target skill levels\nat which training of the skill will be automatically disabled.\n\nHow fast you train a skill is governed by the following factors:\n  - aptitude: Shown <white>white</white> in the Apt column. Higher numbers mean that less\n              experience is needed to advance a skill. Depends on species.\n  - skill manuals: Rare books giving an aptitude bonus of <lightred>+4</lightred> in one skill.\n\nOn top of that, there are some effects which directly modify a skill, for\nexample having trained a related skill (crosstraining), or certain god powers.\nReduced skills are shown in <magenta>magenta</magenta>, and enhanced skills in <green>green</green>.\nThe training percentage (in <brown>brown</brown>) shows the relative amount of experience\nwhich will be used to train this skill.\n\nApart from (de)selecting skills, you also have the following toggles:\n  <w>/</w> :  Switch between automatic and manual training modes.\n  <w>*</w> :  Display all skills, even those which aren't currently useful.\n  <w>?</w> :  Allows you to read the descriptions of skills.\n  <w>_</w> :  Show or hide skill level modifications (god powers, crosstraining).\n  <w>!</w> :  Switch between displaying training percentage, skill cost, or training\n       targets.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"skills and spell library","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/command.cc@\"skill-menu\"#1","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/help.txt@skill-menu","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt@skill-menu","crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"],"fact_sha256":"312cfdc8a5523061f59513ebb64a98ff2cb35b906ac3e4c56becfdc555136141","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"help:skill-menu","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"anchor":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@\"skill-menu\"#1","guards":[],"path":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"                        <h>技能管理</h>\n\n你可以通过杀死怪物（及很少的其他一些活动）获得经验值。\n经验值被用来训练技能。\n在技能界面，你可以选择技能进行训练。\n\n通过按下字母，你可以循环调整是否及如何训练技能：\n  <darkgrey>- 取消选择</darkgrey>  不会训练此技能。\n  + 选择      会训练此技能\n  <w>* 聚焦</w>      此技能在训练时会有更高权重。\n\n按下大写字母可选择此技能并取消选择其他所有技能。\n当你只想训练一种技能时，这个快捷键非常有用。\n\n训练可在两种模式下进行，通过<w>/</w>键切换。\n  - 自动：    技能权重取决于你对它们的使用。\n  - 手动：    经验会被均匀分配给所有激活的技能。\n\n在训练目标模式下，你可以设置技能的训练目标：\n达到目标等级后，系统会自动停用该技能的训练。\n\n训练技能的速度取决于以下因素：\n  - 天赋：在Apt栏中以<white>白色</white>显示。\n数值越高则意味着升级某一技能所需的经验越少。这取决于种族。\n  - 技能手册：这种罕见的书能为一个技能提供<lightred>+4</lightred>的天赋加成。\n\n除此之外，还有一些效果能直接调整技能，\n例如训练了相关技能（交叉训练）或某些神力。\n削弱的技能以<magenta>洋红色</magenta>显示，\n增强的技能以<green>绿色</green>显示。\n训练百分比（以<brown>棕色</brown>显示）表示用于该技能的经验值占比。\n\n除了（取消）选择技能外，还可以进行以下切换：\n  <w>/</w> ：在自动和手动训练模式之间进行切换。\n  <w>*</w> ：显示所有技能，即使是那些目前无用的技能。\n  <w>?</w> ：阅读技能描述。\n  <w>_</w> ：显示或隐藏技能等级的调整（神力、交叉训练）。\n  <w>!</w> ：切换显示训练百分比、技能成本或训练目标。","chinese_key_line":236,"chinese_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</brown>":1,"</darkgrey>":1,"</green>":1,"</h>":1,"</lightred>":1,"</magenta>":1,"</w>":7,"</white>":1,"<brown>":1,"<darkgrey>":1,"<green>":1,"<h>":1,"<lightred>":1,"<magenta>":1,"<w>":7,"<white>":1},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<darkgrey>","</darkgrey>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<white>","</white>","<lightred>","</lightred>","<magenta>","</magenta>","<green>","</green>","<brown>","</brown>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":["4"],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["* 聚焦","/","/","*","?","_","!"]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"dependency_group":"skills and spell library","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","english":"                        <h>Skill management</h>\n\nYou gain experience for killing monsters (and very few other activities). This\nexperience is used to train skills. The skill screen allows you to select which\nskills to train.\n\nBy pressing its letter, you can cycle if and how a skill is trained:\n  <darkgrey>- deselected</darkgrey>    Skill will not be trained.\n  + selected      Skill will be trained.\n  <w>* focused</w>       Skill will be weighted higher for training.\n\nPressing a capital letter selects that skill and deselects all the others.\nThis shortcut is useful when you want to train only one skill.\n\nTraining can be done in two modes, which are toggled with the <w>/</w> key.\n  - automatic:    Skills are weighted based on your use of them.\n  - manual:       Experience is spread evenly across all activated skills.\n\nIn training target mode you can set skill training targets: target skill levels\nat which training of the skill will be automatically disabled.\n\nHow fast you train a skill is governed by the following factors:\n  - aptitude: Shown <white>white</white> in the Apt column. Higher numbers mean that less\n              experience is needed to advance a skill. Depends on species.\n  - skill manuals: Rare books giving an aptitude bonus of <lightred>+4</lightred> in one skill.\n\nOn top of that, there are some effects which directly modify a skill, for\nexample having trained a related skill (crosstraining), or certain god powers.\nReduced skills are shown in <magenta>magenta</magenta>, and enhanced skills in <green>green</green>.\nThe training percentage (in <brown>brown</brown>) shows the relative amount of experience\nwhich will be used to train this skill.\n\nApart from (de)selecting skills, you also have the following toggles:\n  <w>/</w> :  Switch between automatic and manual training modes.\n  <w>*</w> :  Display all skills, even those which aren't currently useful.\n  <w>?</w> :  Allows you to read the descriptions of skills.\n  <w>_</w> :  Show or hide skill level modifications (god powers, crosstraining).\n  <w>!</w> :  Switch between displaying training percentage, skill cost, or training\n       targets.","english_key_line":245,"english_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</brown>":1,"</darkgrey>":1,"</green>":1,"</h>":1,"</lightred>":1,"</magenta>":1,"</w>":7,"</white>":1,"<brown>":1,"<darkgrey>":1,"<green>":1,"<h>":1,"<lightred>":1,"<magenta>":1,"<w>":7,"<white>":1},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<darkgrey>","</darkgrey>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<white>","</white>","<lightred>","</lightred>","<magenta>","</magenta>","<green>","</green>","<brown>","</brown>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":["4"],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["* glossary 变化仅涉及不相关的独特怪物称号域。当前 identity、生命周期、生产行为和结构证据已按稳定 locator 重新核对，无需进一步改动。原 adjust 理由：基线漏掉目标达成后的自动停用含义，并把若干显示名译得不精确；局部调整即可。","terminal_conclusion":"keep"}
{"actual_behavior":"法术库用 Tab/! 与 Shift-Tab 双向切换记忆、描述、隐藏、显示页面；Ctrl-F 过滤并可用空输入清除过滤器。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"<h>法术库</h>\n<w>Tab</w>或<w>!</w>  ：正向循环切换<w>记忆</w>、<w>描述</w>、<w>隐藏</w>和<w>显示</w>页面。\n<w>Shift-Tab</w> ：反向循环切换这些页面。\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>    ：使用正则表达式筛选显示的法术。\n<w>?</w>         ：显示此帮助。\n\n默认情况下，法术库会打开<w>记忆</w>页面；要开始记忆某个法术，只需按其字母。\n要阅读法术描述，请先按<w>Tab</w>切换到<w>描述</w>页面，再按该法术的字母。\n\n大多数角色不会想记忆法术库中积累的大多数法术；\n这时<w>显示</w>和<w>隐藏</w>页面就很有用。\n要从<w>记忆</w>和<w>描述</w>页面隐藏某个法术，\n请在<w>隐藏</w>页面按该法术的字母。\n被隐藏的法术可以在<w>显示</w>页面重新显示。\n\n<w>auto_hide_spells</w>选项可能很有用；启用后，新发现的法术默认都会隐藏。\n\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>可用于筛选法术列表。\n以这种方式设置的筛选条件会一直保留，直到关闭并重新打开法术库，\n或再次按<w>Ctrl-F</w>设置新的筛选条件。\n如果在提示处不输入任何文本直接按<w>Enter</w>，就会清除筛选。\n你可以按法术名称和法术学派筛选法术。","current_english":"<h>Spell Library</h>\n<w>Tab</w> or <w>!</w>  : Cycle forward through <w>Memorise</w>, <w>Describe</w>, <w>Hide</w>, and <w>Show</w> pages.\n<w>Shift-Tab</w> : Cycle backward through these pages.\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>    : Filter the list of spells shown with a regex.\n<w>?</w>         : Show this help.\n\nBy default, the spell library opens to the <w>Memorise</w> page; to begin memorising a\nspell, just press its letter. To read a spell's description, first switch to\nthe <w>Describe</w> page by pressing <w>Tab</w>, then press the spell's letter.\n\nMost characters will not be interested in memorising the majority of spells that\naccumulate in their spell library; this is where the <w>Show</w> and <w>Hide</w> pages come in\nhandy. To hide a spell from the <w>Memorise</w> and <w>Describe</w> pages, press its spell\nletter from the <w>Hide</w> page. Hidden spells can be shown again from the <w>Show</w> page.\n\nThe <w>auto_hide_spells</w> option can be useful; when enabled, all newly discovered\nspells are hidden by default.\n\n<w>Ctrl-F</w> can be used to filter the list of spells. Any filter set in this way will\npersist until the spell library is closed and reopened, or until a new filter is\nset with <w>Ctrl-F</w>. If <w>Enter</w> is pressed at the prompt without entering any text,\nthat will reset the filter to none.\nYou can filter spells by name and by spell school.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"skills and spell library","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help {"actual_behavior":"储藏搜索支持名称、正则、属性、地标、范围和逻辑运算符，并可从结果查看物品/商店或前往位置。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"<h>搜索储藏追踪器</h>\n\n你可以按名称（或名称中的子字符串）搜索物品和地牢地标。\n在搜索结果列表中，按搜索结果对应的热键，即可选择该结果并前往其位置。\n\n搜索字符串示例：\n<w>mutation</w>      找出所有变异药水，包括商店中的变异药水。\n<w>throwing net</w>  找出每一张单独的投网以及成堆的投网。\n<w>shop</w>          列出所有商店。按<w>!</w>，再按商店对应的字母即可查看库存。\n<w>Lair:2</w>        找出已知位于兽巢第 2 层的所有东西。\n<w>/D:[2-4]</w>      找出地牢第 2 至 4 层的所有东西。可以使用正则表达式！\n              注意，/D:[3-10]不会按预期工作，因为[x-y]表示\n              正则表达式中的字符范围。\n<w>rPois</w>         找出所有具有rPois抗性的物品。\n<w>rC++</w>          找出所有具有rC++抗性的物品。\n<w>+3</w>            找出所有具有+3附魔的物品。\n<w>/Lair.*axe</w>和<w>axe && Lair</w>都会显示兽巢中的所有斧头。\n\n快捷方式：\n<w>.</w>             列出当前层的所有东西。\n<w>..</w>            列出你已知的所有物品。\n<w>0</w>-<w>9</w>        列出指定导航点上的所有物品。\n<w>*</w>             列出所有导航点上的所有物品。\n<w>@<<query></w>      列出当前层所有与<<query>匹配的东西。\n<w>/<<regex></w>      将<<regex>作为正则表达式，列出与之匹配的物品。\n<w>=<<string></w>     列出包含<<string>这一子字符串的物品。\n\n在搜索结果中按<w>?</w>，再按搜索结果对应的热键，还可以查看商店和物品。\n系统会显示物品描述或商店库存。\n\n储藏搜索字符串不区分大小写。\n\n<h>按物品属性搜索：</h>\n\n你可以搜索法术名称（如<w>Ignite Poison</w>），找出包含该法术的法术书。\n\n你可以搜索神器属性（如<w>/prevents.*teleport</w>），找出具有该属性的神器。\n\n你还可以按物品的其他属性搜索：\n<w>artefact</w>或<w>artifact</w>会找出已鉴定的神器；\n<w>ego</w>或<w>branded</w>会找出带有铭印的非神器，以及尚未鉴定、可能带有铭印的物品；\n<w>throwable</w>会找出可以投掷的物品；\n<w>dropped</w>会找出你丢弃过的物品；\n<w>in_shop</w>会找出商店中的物品；\n而（若启用了autopickup_search选项）<w>autopickup</w>会找出会被自动拾取的物品。\n\n技能名称（如<w>Polearms</w>或<w>Long Blades</w>）会找出可训练该技能的所有武器。\n你还可以搜索<w>melee weapon</w>和<w>ranged weapon</w>。\n\n你也可以按物品类型搜索，如<w>book</w>、<w>manual</w>或<w>jewellery</w>。\n对于护甲槽位，可以使用<w>helmet</w>（所有头部护具）、<w>gloves</w>、\n<w>shield</w>和<w>body</w>。\n使用<w>armour && !!body</w>会列出除躯干护甲以外的所有护甲。\n\n<h>查找地牢地标：</h>\n\n你可以按名称搜索地牢地标：搜索<w>shop</w>会找到所有商店\n（包括名称中不含“shop”的商店）；其他地牢地标也可按名称找到，\n如<w>dispersal trap</w>、<w>altar</w>等。\n你还可以按神祇名称搜索祭坛：<w>辛</w>。\n\n<h>非正则表达式运算符：</h>\n\n可以用!!对搜索字符串取反，用&&表示逻辑与，用||表示逻辑或，\n并用<<<< >>分组。\n\n例如，要查找戒指（首饰）而不是环甲，可以使用：<w>ring && !!mail</w>。\n\n请注意，<w>/</w>和<w>=</w>只作用于单个模式，而不是整个查询：\n应当搜索<w>armour && /r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>，\n而不是<w>/armour && r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>。","current_english":"<h>Searching the stash-tracker</h>\n\nYou can search for items and dungeon features by name (or a substring of the\nname). In the list of search results, you can select a search result by its\nhotkey to travel to its location.\n\nSome examples of search strings:\n<w>mutation</w>      find all potions of mutation, including potions in shops.\n<w>throwing net</w>  finds every single throwing net and piles of nets.\n<w>shop</w>          lists all shops. Press <w>!</w> and a shop's letter for the stock.\n<w>Lair:2</w>        find everything known to be on Lair:2.\n<w>/D:[2-4]</w>      finds everything on Dungeon levels 2-4. Regexes are allowed!\n              Note that /D:[3-10] will not work as intended, since [x-y] is a\n              regex character range.\n<w>rPois</w>         finds all items with an rPois resistance.\n<w>rC++</w>          finds all items with an rC++ resistance.\n<w>+3</w>            finds all items with a +3 enchantment.\n<w>/Lair.*axe</w> and <w>axe && Lair</w> both show all axes in the Lair.\n\nSome shortcuts:\n<w>.</w>             lists everything on your current level.\n<w>..</w>            lists all items you know.\n<w>0</w> to <w>9</w>        lists all items at the specified waypoint.\n<w>*</w>             lists all items at all waypoints.\n<w>@<<query></w>      lists everything matching <<query> on your current level.\n<w>/<<regex></w>      lists items matching <<regex> as a regular expression.\n<w>=<<string></w>     lists items containing <<string> as a substring.\n\nYou can also examine shops and items in the search results by pressing <w>?</w> and\nthen selecting the hotkey for the search result. This will give a description\nof the item or the contents of the shop.\n\nThe stash search string is case-insensitive.\n\n<h>Searching by item properties:</h>\n\nYou can search for spell names (such as <w>Ignite Poison</w>) to find spellbooks\nthat contain the spell.\n\nYou can search for artefact item properties (such as <w>/prevents.*teleport</w>)\nto find artefacts that have the property.\n\nYou can search by some additional item properties: <w>artefact</w> or\n<w>artifact</w> will find identified artefacts, <w>ego</w> or <w>branded</w>\nwill find non-artefacts with a brand and unidentified items which may be\nbranded, <w>throwable</w> will find things you can throw, <w>dropped</w> will\nfind items you have dropped, <w>in_shop</w> will find items in shops, and (if\nthe autopickup_search option is enabled) <w>autopickup</w> will find items that\nwould be automatically picked up.\n\nSkill names (such as <w>Polearms</w> or <w>Long Blades</w>) will find all weapons that\ntrain that skill. You can also look for <w>melee weapon</w> and <w>ranged weapon</w>.\n\nYou can also search by item types such as <w>book</w> or <w>manual</w> or <w>jewellery</w>.\nFor armour slots, use <w>helmet</w> (all headgear), <w>gloves</w>, <w>shield</w>, and <w>body</w>.\nUse <w>armour && !!body</w> to list all armour pieces except for body armour.\n\n<h>Finding Dungeon Features:</h>\n\nYou can search for dungeon features by name: all shops will be found by a search\nfor <w>shop</w> (including shops that do not have \"shop\" in their name); other\ndungeon features can also be found by name: <w>dispersal trap</w>, <w>altar</w>,\netc. You can also search for altars by deity name: <w>Zin</w>.\n\n<h>Non-regex operators:</h>\n\nYou can use !! to negate a search string, && for a boolean AND, || for\nboolean OR, and <<<< >> for grouping.\n\nFor instance, to find rings (jewellery) but not ring mail you could use:\n<w>ring && !!mail</w>\n\nNote that <w>/</w> and <w>=</w> apply to individual patterns, not the entire query:\nyou should search for <w>armour && /r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>, not\n<w>/armour && r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"map, search, travel, and annotation","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/command.cc@\"stash-search.prompt\"#1","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/help.txt@stash-search.prompt","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt@stash-search.prompt","crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for Press <w>!</w> and a shop's letter for the stock.\n<w>Lair:2</w>        find everything known to be on Lair:2.\n<w>/D:[2-4]</w>      finds everything on Dungeon levels 2-4. This will give a description\nof the item or the contents of the shop.\n\nThe stash search string is case-insensitive.\n\n<h>Searching by item properties:</h>\n\nYou can search for spell names (such as <w>Ignite Poison</w>) to find spellbooks\nthat contain the spell.\n\nYou can search for artefact item properties (such as <w>/prevents.*teleport</w>)\nto find artefacts that have the property.\n\nYou can search by some additional item properties: <w>artefact</w> or\n<w>artifact</w> will find identified artefacts, <w>ego</w> or <w>branded</w>\nwill find non-artefacts with a brand and unidentified items which may be\nbranded, <w>throwable</w> will find things you can throw, <w>dropped</w> will\nfind items you have dropped, <w>in_shop</w> will find items in shops, and (if\nthe autopickup_search option is enabled) <w>autopickup</w> will find items that\nwould be automatically picked up.\n\nSkill names (such as <w>Polearms</w> or <w>Long Blades</w>) will find all weapons that\ntrain that skill.
