{"actual_behavior":"巫师怪物规格支持物种、装备、命名、属性、召唤与法术槽；法术标志以生产 parser 实际支持的集合和沉默、反魔法、噪音、距离行为为准。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"current_chinese":"<h>生成指定怪物</h>\n\n要生成带有其物种默认装备的怪物，只需输入物种名称，\n例如<w>kobold</w>或<w>Yiuf</w>。\n\n可以把hydra或slymdra生成为指定头数，分别写作\nn<w>-headed</w> <w>hydra</w>或n<w>-headed</w> <w>slymdra</w>（头数为1至20）：\n\n<w>four-headed hydra</w>\n\n要设置（极）丑物刚生成时的颜色，可使用：\n\n<w>white very ugly thing</w>\n\n有效颜色为<w>brown</w>、<w>cyan</w>、<w>green</w>、<w>purple</w>、\n<w>red</w>和<w>white</w>。\n\n要定制变异兽，可在其名称前加上成熟度和/或一组以连字符分隔的特征：\n\n<w>mature shock-weird mutant beast</w>\n\n有效成熟度为<w>larval</w>、<w>juvenile</w>、<w>mature</w>、\n<w>elder</w>和<w>primal</w>。特征为<w>sting</w>、<w>bat</w>、<w>fire</w>、\n<w>weird</w>、<w>shock</w>和<w>ox</w>。\n\n要让黏液生物使用非正常体型，可加上前缀<w>large</w>、\n<w>very large</w>、<w>enormous</w>或<w>titanic</w>：\n\n<w>very large slime creature</w>\n\n如果怪物属于某种怪物群，可以在名称中加入关键词<w>band</w>，\n请求让它有资格带上同群成员。例如：\n\n<w>orc warlord band</w>\n\n指定<w>band</w>不会强制放置怪物群，只会让游戏按正常概率尝试生成。\n只有空间足够时才会放置怪物群。\n\n要给怪物指定物品，可在怪物名称后加分号，再接物品列表。例如：\n\n<w>orc ; rapier | quick blade . chain mail | plate armour</w>\n\n这会生成一个持握刺剑或迅捷之刃、穿戴锁子甲或板甲的兽人。\n只有明确请求时才会生成随机神器或附魔装备。\n请注意，怪物最初生成时携带的所有物品都会被移除并销毁。\n也可以借此强制怪物完全不携带物品：\n\n<w>orc; nothing</w>\n\n更多信息请参阅<cyan>docs/develop/levels/syntax.txt</cyan>中的\n<cyan>ITEMS</cyan>一节。这里的斜线（<w>/</w>）都应替换为竖线（<w>|</w>），\n逗号（<w>,</w>）都应替换为句点（<w>.</w>）。\n\n同一语法也可用于生成特定类型的舞动武器：\n\n<w>dancing weapon; rapier</w>\n\n限制：如果给怪物随机生成发射武器，就无法强制弹药类型与发射武器匹配。\n但有一个特例：舞动武器若为发射武器，总会同时生成一些合适的弹药。\n\n使用标签<w>always_corpse</w>可指定怪物必定生成尸体，\n使用<w>never_corpse</w>则可指定它绝不生成尸体。\n\n怪物默认在睡眠状态下生成。在名称前加上<w>generate_awake</w>可改变这一点。\n\n<w>generate_awake boulder beetle</w>\n\n前缀<w>patrolling</w>会阻止怪物走出其生成地点的视野范围。\n\n可以用<w>god</w>:说明符设置怪物的神祇：\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one</w>\n\n如果该怪物应当是指定神祇赐予的礼物，还需在其后加上<w>god_gift</w>：\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one god_gift</w>\n\n请注意，只有明确指定<w>god_gift</w>，怪物才会被视为神祇赠礼。\n\n可以用<w>att</w>:指定怪物的阵营，取值如下：\n\n* <w>att:hostile</w> -- 默认\n* <w>att:friendly</w> -- 驯服，会跟随你\n* <w>att:neutral</w> -- 对你和att:hostile怪物都抱有敌意\n* <w>att:good_neutral</w> -- 对att:hostile怪物抱有敌意\n* <w>att:fellow_slime</w> -- 驯服，但不会跟随你\n\n可以给怪物指定颜色，覆盖其默认颜色：\n\n<w>col:darkgrey fungus</w>\n\n也可以指定颜色<w>any</w>，此时会随机选择颜色。\n\n可以用<w>tile</w>:说明符覆盖怪物显示的贴图：\n\n<w>rat tile:mons_giant_bat</w>\n\n在贴图版中，这会生成一个使用“<cyan>mons_giant_bat</cyan>”贴图、\n其他方面与老鼠完全相同的怪物。在ASCII模式中，这项设置不起作用。\n\n要放置适合当前地图生成楼层的随机怪物，可以使用：\n\n<w>random</w>\n\n要放置适合其他地点的随机怪物，可以在怪物规格中使用place:标签：\n\n<w>place:Abyss</w>\n\n使用<w>place</w>:即表示要生成随机怪物。\n也可以请求把适合其他深度的随机怪物生成为亡灵，例如：\n\n<w>place:Elf:3 zombie</w>\n\n可用修饰语为<w>zombie</w>、<w>draugr</w>、<w>simulacrum</w>和<w>spectre</w>。\n\n<h>覆盖怪物名称</h>\n\n可以按如下方式给单个怪物命名：\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent</w>\n\n这会让怪物显示为“<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>”。\n名称中的空格可以用_符号代替。\n值得注意的是，“<cyan>the <<race></cyan>”将被附加到所有名称上，\n因此构思名称时应考虑这一点。\n\n名称可以使用三种不同的修饰标签：<w>name_adjective</w>、\n<w>name_suffix</w>和<w>name_replace</w>。<w>name_adjective</w>会让名称充当形容词。例如：\n\n<w>kobold name:ugly name_adjective</w>\n\n会产生“<cyan>An ugly kobold</cyan>”“<cyan>The ugly kobold hits you</cyan>”等文本。\n\n<w>name_suffix</w>的作用相同，但名称会放在怪物基础名称之后：\n\n<w>kobold name:wearing_mittens name_suffix</w>\n\n会产生“<cyan>A kobold wearing mittens</cyan>”\n“<cyan>The kobold wearing mittens hits you</cyan>”等文本。\n\n<w>name_replace</w>会用给定名称替换基础名称，如同该怪物是独有怪物：\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent name_replace</w>\n\n结果是“<cyan>Durwent</cyan>”而不是“<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>”，\n但尸体仍会显示为“<cyan>kobold corpse of Durwent</cyan>”。\n\n<w>name_species</w>会让尸体原样使用该名称；与<w>name_replace</w>一起使用时，\n不会在尸体名称前加上“<cyan>kobold</cyan> <cyan>corpse of X</cyan>”。\n在本地贴图版中，它还会避免在独有怪物和改名怪物上方显示通常的名称标签。\n\n与<w>name_replace</w>或<w>name_species</w>组合使用时，还有两个标签可用于\n重命名怪物、但不赋予怪物实际的“名字”：<w>name_descriptor</w>和<w>name_definite</w>。\n\n例如：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace</w>\n\n这会产生“<cyan>goblin hits you</cyan>”。但加入<w>name_descriptor</w>后，\n会产生“<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>”，怪物描述则会显示为\n“<cyan>A goblin</cyan>”。\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor</w>\n\n<w>name_definite</w>标签确保怪物绝不会使用“<cyan>a</cyan>”或\n“<cyan>an</cyan>”，而是始终使用定冠词。因此会显示\n“<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>”，怪物描述也会显示为\n“<cyan>The goblin</cyan>”。设置<w>name_definite</w>会自动设置\n<w>name_descriptor</w>，所以：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_definite</w>\n\n完全等同于：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor name_definite</w>\n\n<w>name_zombie</w>可用于重新定义已经亡灵化的怪物\n（包括draugr（尸鬼）、拟像和幽魂）。\n\n最后，<w>name_nocorpse</w>会让尸体完全忽略该名称。例如：\n\n<w>wizard name:sad name_adjective name_nocorpse</w>\n\n这会让“<cyan>a sad wizard</cyan>”留下“<cyan>a wizard corpse</cyan>”。\n\n如果定义中的空间有限，可以使用这些标签的缩写：\n\n* <w>name_suffix</w> -> <w>n_suf</w>\n* <w>name_adjective</w> -> <w>n_adj</w>\n* <w>name_replace</w> -> <w>n_rpl</w>\n* <w>name_species</w> -> <w>n_spe</w>\n* <w>name_descriptor</w> -> <w>n_des</w>\n* <w>name_definite</w> -> <w>n_the</w>\n* <w>name_zombie</w> -> <w>n_zom</w>\n* <w>name_nocorpse</w> -> <w>n_noc</w>\n\n<h>覆盖怪物属性</h>\n\n可以用<w>hd</w>和<w>hp</w>进一步定制怪物。\n这两个标签能完全覆盖怪物原有的生命骰和生命值：\n\n<w>kobold hd:20</w>\n\n这会生成一个生命骰为20的狗头人。它的HP会根据生命骰随机生成，\n因此修改HD后也会按新的HD重新掷出HP。显式指定了HP的怪物，其HP不会因此改变。\n\n也可以显式指定生命值：\n\n<w>kobold hp:20</w>\n\n这会生成一个生命值为20的狗头人。\n这对生命值原本不随机的怪物（如雕像）也很有用。\n\n可以用<w>dur</w>和<w>sum</w>标签把怪物标记为召唤物。\n<w>dur</w>标签接受1到6（含）之间的整数。<w>sum</w>标签接受法术名称\n（按下文所述的<w>spells</w>标签解析），也接受以下任一值：\n<w>clone</w>（Rakshasa，罗刹）、<w>animate</w>（来自“操纵死尸”）、\n<w>chaos</w>（由纯粹混沌生成）、<w>miscast</w>（由施法失误效果召唤）、\n<w>zot</w>（由佐特陷阱召唤）、<w>wrath</w>（神祇降下惩罚时召唤）、\n<w>aid</w>（神祇提供援助时召唤）。\n\n在法术中，<w>shadow_creatures</w>（暗影生物）的影响最明显：\n怪物会消散在暗影中，而不是化作一团烟雾消失。使用非召唤法术则不会产生效果。\n\n给怪物加上<w>seen</w>标签会覆盖系统设置，强制把它标记为已经见过；\n因此不会产生“<cyan>A kobold comes into view</cyan>”之类的消息。\n\n<h>覆盖怪物法术</h>\n\n可以用<w>spells</w>:标签覆盖怪物的法术集：\n\n*<w>goblin spells:throw_flame.50.wizard</w>\n*<w>ancient lich spells:symbol_of_torment.26.wizard; glaciate.51.wizard</w>\n\n（这是法术槽列表。每个槽由法术名称、施法频率和随后指定的法术槽标志组成；\n法术名称中的空格用下划线替换，各部分以<w>.</w>分隔，法术槽以<w>;</w>分隔。\n<w>;</w>两侧以及<w>spells</w>:前缀之后都不能有空格。）\n\n法术名称必须与<cyan>spl-data.h</cyan>中的名称完全一致，空格用下划线替换。\n法术频率表示施放该法术的概率（x/200）。法术标志包括：\n\n* <w>natural</w>、<w>magical</w>、<w>vocal</w>、<w>wizard</w>或\n<w>priest</w> —— 施放法术的方式。wizard、priest和vocal法术受沉默影响；\nwizard和priest法术会触发相应的\n神祇行为判定（特洛格会赞赏杀死巫师，比欧弗会赞赏杀死祭司）。\nmagical和wizard法术受禁魔效果影响。\nnatural和magical法术默认无声，除非标有noisy。\n* <w>emergency</w> —— 怪物只会在生命值较低时施放该法术。\n* <w>breath</w> —— 该法术是吐息武器；使用后会触发吐息冷却，\n冷却期间不能再次施放该法术。\n* <w>short_range</w> —— 仅在目标距离较近时施放该法术。\n* <w>long_range</w> —— 仅在目标距离较远时施放该法术。\n* <w>instant</w> —— 该法术不消耗怪物的能量；怪物可在同一回合采取另一个行动。\n* <w>noisy</w> —— 强制该法术发出声响，即使它属于默认无声的类型。\n\n可以用<w>none</w>指定法术槽为空。要强制生成无法术怪物，可使用：\n\n<w>orc wizard spells:none</w>\n\n<h>设置附魔效果</h>\n\n可以给怪物添加任意数量的附魔效果。怪物可以在生成时处于加速、失明状态，\n具有永久 RMsl 等。语法为<w>ench:<<name>:<<duration>:<<degree></w>或\n<w>perm_ench:<<name>:<<degree></w>。\n<w><<degree></w>和<w><<duration></w>字段均可省略。\n\n例如：\n\n* <w>orc perm_ench:blind perm_ench:mad</w>\n* <w>dream sheep ench:sticky_flame</w>\n* <w>stone giant dur:1 ench:berserk</w>\n","current_english":"<h>Create specified monster</h>\n\nTo create a monster with the default equipment for its species, simply enter\nthe species name, such as <w>kobold</w> or <w>Yiuf</w>.\n\nA hydra or slymdra can be given a specific number of heads by calling it an\nn<w>-headed</w> <w>hydra</w> or n<w>-headed</w> <w>slymdra</w> (with a maximum of 20 heads):\n\n<w>four-headed hydra</w>\n\nTo set the colour a (very) ugly thing takes when it is first created, use:\n\n<w>white very ugly thing</w>\n\nThe valid colours are <w>brown</w>, <w>cyan</w>, <w>green</w>, <w>purple</w>, <w>red</w> and <w>white</w>.\n\nA mutant beast can be customised by prefacing it with a maturity level and/or\nhyphen-separated set of traits, as follows:\n\n<w>mature shock-weird mutant beast</w>\n\nThe valid maturity levels are <w>larval</w>, <w>juvenile</w>, <w>mature</w>, <w>elder</w> and <w>primal</w>. The\nfacets are <w>sting</w>, <w>bat</w>, <w>fire</w>, <w>weird</w>, <w>shock</w> and <w>ox</w>.\n\nA slime creature's size can be set to other than normal using the prefixes\n<w>large</w>, <w>very large</w>, <w>enormous</w> or <w>titanic</w>:\n\n<w>very large slime creature</w>\n\nIf a monster is a member of a band, you can request that it be eligible for\nband members by adding the keyword <w>band</w> to the name. For instance:\n\n<w>orc warlord band</w>\n\nSpecifying <w>band</w> doesn't force bands to be placed - it requests that the game\nuse the normal chances of creating a band. A band will only be placed if there\nis space for it.\n\nA monster can be given specific items by following the monster name with a\nsemi-colon and then with an item list. For example:\n\n<w>orc ; rapier | quick blade . chain mail | plate armour</w>\n\nwill generate an orc wielding either a rapier or a quick blade and wearing\neither chain mail or plate armour. Randarts and ego items are only generated\nif they are explicitly requested. Note that any items that the monster was\noriginally generated with will be removed and destroyed. This can be used to\nforce a monster to have no items whatsoever:\n\n<w>orc; nothing</w>\n\nFor further information, see <cyan>docs/develop/levels/syntax.txt</cyan>, section <cyan>ITEMS</cyan>.\nAny reference to a slash (<w>/</w>) should be replaced with a pipe (<w>|</w>) and any comma\n(<w>,</w>) with a full stop (<w>.</w>).\n\nThe same syntax is also used to create a specific type of dancing weapon:\n\n<w>dancing weapon; rapier</w>\n\nLimitations: If a monster is given a random launcher, there is no way to force\nthe ammo type to match the launcher type. As a special case, however, a\nlauncher dancing weapon is always generated with some suitable ammunition.\n\nYou can specify that a monster will always generate a corpse by using the tag\n<w>always_corpse</w>, or that it will never generate a corpse with <w>never_corpse</w>.\n\nBy default, monsters are generated asleep. This is changed by prefixing the\nname with <w>generate_awake</w>.\n\n<w>generate_awake boulder beetle</w>\n\nA prefix of <w>patrolling</w> prevents it from walking out of sight of the place\nwhere it was created.\n\nYou can set the monster's god using the <w>god</w>: specifier as follows:\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one</w>\n\nIf the monster is to be a gift of the specified god, this should be suffixed\nwith <w>god_gift</w>:\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one god_gift</w>\n\nNote that the monster will not be considered a god gift unless <w>god_gift</w> is\nexplicitly set.\n\nYou can set the monster's faction by specifying <w>att</w>: one of:\n\n* <w>att:hostile</w> -- the default\n* <w>att:friendly</w> -- tame, will follow you\n* <w>att:neutral</w> -- hostile to both you and att:hostile monsters\n* <w>att:good_neutral</w> -- hostile to att:hostile\n* <w>att:fellow_slime</w> -- tame, won't follow you\n\nMonsters can be given colours that override their default colour:\n\n<w>col:darkgrey fungus</w>\n\nThe colour <w>any</w> can be given, in which case a random colour will be chosen.\n\nYou can override the displayed monster tile using the <w>tile</w>: specifier as\nfollows:\n\n<w>rat tile:mons_giant_bat</w>\n\nIn tiles version, this would create a monster that uses the \"<cyan>mons_giant_bat</cyan>\"\ntile, but is otherwise identical to a rat. In ASCII mode, this will do\nnothing.\n\nIf you want to place a random monster suitable for the level the map is\ngenerated on, you can use\n\n<w>random</w>\n\nIf you want to place a random monster suitable for some other place, you can\nuse a place: tag in the monster spec:\n\n<w>place:Abyss</w>\n\nUsing <w>place</w>: implies that you want a random monster. You can also request\nzombies from random monsters suitable for some other depth as (e.g.):\n\n<w>place:Elf:3 zombie</w>\n\nThe available modifiers are <w>zombie</w>, <w>draugr</w>, <w>simulacrum</w>, and <w>spectre</w>.\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Names</h>\n\nIndividual monsters can be given names as follows:\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent</w>\n\nThis will cause the monster to appear as \"<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>\". Spaces can be\nplaced in the name by substituting them with the _ symbol. It is worth noting\nthat \"<cyan>the <<race></cyan>\" will be appended to all names, which should be considered\nwhen coming up with them.\n\nThere are three different modifiers that can be used on a name:\n<w>name_adjective</w>, <w>name_suffix</w> and <w>name_replace</w>. <w>name_adjective</w> makes the\nname act as an adjective. For example:\n\n<w>kobold name:ugly name_adjective</w>\n\nWill cause \"<cyan>An ugly kobold</cyan>\", \"<cyan>The ugly kobold hits you</cyan>\", and so on.\n\n<w>name_suffix</w> does the same, but after the monster's base name:\n\n<w>kobold name:wearing_mittens name_suffix</w>\n\nWill give \"<cyan>A kobold wearing mittens</cyan>\", \"<cyan>The kobold wearing mittens hits you</cyan>\",\nand so on.\n\n<w>name_replace</w> causes the base name to be replaced by given name, as if the\nmonster was a unique:\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent name_replace</w>\n\nWill result in \"<cyan>Durwent</cyan>\" rather than \"<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>\". The corpse will\nstill be a \"<cyan>kobold corpse of Durwent</cyan>\".\n\n<w>name_species</w> uses the name for corpses as-is, without prefixing it with\n\"<cyan>kobold</cyan> <cyan>corpse of X</cyan>\" for <w>name_replace</w>. It also avoids the usual name tag\nthat displays above unique and renamed monsters in local tiles.\n\nIn combination with <w>name_replace</w> or <w>name_species</w>, there are two further tags\nthat can be used when renaming monsters, but not giving them an actual \"name\":\n<w>name_descriptor</w>, and <w>name_definite</w>.\n\nFor example:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace</w>\n\nWill result in \"<cyan>goblin hits you</cyan>\". However, adding <w>name_descriptor</w> from the\nabove flags will result in \"<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>\" and in the description of the\nmonster, \"<cyan>A goblin</cyan>\".\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor</w>\n\nThe <w>name_definite</w> flag ensures that it is never referred to as\n\"<cyan>a</cyan>\" or \"<cyan>an</cyan>\", but that the definite article is always\nused. Therefore, you will see \"<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>\", as per above,\nbut also \"<cyan>The goblin</cyan>\" in its description.\nSetting <w>name_definite</w> will automatically set the flag <w>name_descriptor</w>,\ntherefore meaning that:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_definite</w>\n\nis exactly the same as:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor name_definite</w>\n\n<w>name_zombie</w> can be used to redefine already zombified monsters (including\ndraugr, simulacra and spectrals).\n\nFinally, <w>name_nocorpse</w> will cause the name to be completely ignored for\ncorpses. For example:\n\n<w>wizard name:sad name_adjective name_nocorpse</w>\n\nwill result in \"<cyan>a sad wizard</cyan>\" that leaves \"<cyan>a wizard corpse</cyan>\".\n\nFor instances where space in definitions is limited, you can use abbreviated\nforms of these tags:\n\n* <w>name_suffix</w> -> <w>n_suf</w>\n* <w>name_adjective</w> -> <w>n_adj</w>\n* <w>name_replace</w> -> <w>n_rpl</w>\n* <w>name_species</w> -> <w>n_spe</w>\n* <w>name_descriptor</w> -> <w>n_des</w>\n* <w>name_definite</w> -> <w>n_the</w>\n* <w>name_zombie</w> -> <w>n_zom</w>\n* <w>name_nocorpse</w> -> <w>n_noc</w>\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Stats</h>\n\nFurther monster customisation can be made by using <w>hd</w> and <w>hp</w>. These two tags\nallow you to completely overwrite the original hit dice and hit points of a\nmonster:\n\n<w>kobold hd:20</w>\n\nCreates a kobold with a hit dice of 20. As its hp is randomised, based on its\nhit dice, this will also be re-rolled. Monsters with a specific hit point\nvalue will not have this value change.\n\nYou can also have a specific value for hit points:\n\n<w>kobold hp:20</w>\n\nCreates a kobold with 20 hit points. This is also useful for monsters (such as\nstatues) which come with non-randomised HP values.\n\nMonsters can be marked as summoned by using <w>dur</w> and <w>sum</w> tags. The <w>dur</w> tag\naccepts an integer between 1 and 6 inclusive. The <w>sum</w> tag accepts the name of\na spell (parsed as per the <w>spells</w> tag, described below) or any of <w>clone</w>\n(Rakshasa), <w>animate</w> (from animate dead), <w>chaos</w> (made from pure chaos), <w>miscast</w>\n(summoned from by spell miscast effect), <w>zot</w> (summoned by a Zot trap), <w>wrath</w>\n(summoned by a god in an act of retribution), <w>aid</w> (summoned by a god in order\nto give aid).\n\nOf the spells, <w>shadow_creatures</w> has the most significant effect, in that\nmonsters will dissipate into the shadows rather than disappear in a puff of\nsmoke. Using non-summoning spells will also have no effect.\n\nTagging a monster with <w>seen</w> will override the system and force that monster to\nbe marked as already viewed; this means that it won't generate messages such\nas \"<cyan>A kobold comes into view</cyan>\".\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Spells</h>\n\nMonster spell sets can be overridden with a <w>spells</w>: tag, used as follows:\n\n*<w>goblin spells:throw_flame.50.wizard</w>\n*<w>ancient lich spells:symbol_of_torment.26.wizard; glaciate.51.wizard</w>\n\n(a list of spell slots, consisting of a spell name with spaces replaced with\nunderscores, a frequency of casting and set of spell slot flags specified\nafterwards with <w>.</w>, with slots separated by <w>;</w>. There should be no spaces around\nthe <w>;</w> or after the <w>spells</w>: prefix)\n\nSpell names must exactly match the names in <cyan>spl-data.h</cyan>, with spaces replaced\nby underscores. Spell frequencies are an (x in 200) chance that that spell\nwill be cast. Spell flags include:\n\n* <w>natural</w>, <w>magical</w>, <w>vocal</w>, <w>wizard</w>, or <w>priest</w> - the manner in which this\nspell is being cast. Wizard, priest, and vocal spells are affected by silence.\nWizards and priests trigger appropriate conducts (Trog will appreciate killing\nwizards, Beogh will appreciate killing priests). Magical and wizard spells are\nsubject to antimagic effects. Natural and magical spells do not generate noise\nunless marked <w>noisy</w>.\n* <w>emergency</w> - the monster will only cast the spell when low on health.\n* <w>breath</w> - the spell is a breath weapon, and using it triggers a breath\ntimeout during which the spell cannot be cast again.\n* <w>instant</w> - the spell does not expend the monster's energy; they may take\nanother action on the same turn.\n* <w>noisy</w> - forces the spell to make noise even if it is a natural or magical\nability.\n* <w>short_range</w> - the monster will only cast the spell when its target is at\nshort range.\n* <w>long_range</w> - the monster will only cast the spell when its target is at\nlong range.\n\nYou may use <w>none</w> to specify that a slot should be left empty. You can force a\nspell-less monster with:\n\n<w>orc wizard spells:none</w>\n\n<h>Setting enchantments</h>\n\nYou may give a monster any number of enchantments. It may start hasted, blind,\nhave permanent RMsl, etc. The syntax is <w>ench:<<name>:<<duration>:<<degree></w> or\n<w>perm_ench:<<name>:<<degree></w>. The <w><<degree></w> and <w><<duration></w> fields may be\nomitted.\n\nFor example:\n\n* <w>orc perm_ench:blind perm_ench:mad</w>\n* <w>dream sheep ench:sticky_flame</w>\n* <w>stone giant dur:1 ench:berserk</w>\n","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"macros, platform keys, and wizard","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/wiz-mon.cc@\"wiz-monster\"#1","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/help.txt@wiz-monster","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt@wiz-monster","crawl-ref/source/command.cc@for (const string &line : split_string(\"\\n\", help, false, true))\n            formatted_lines.push_back(formatted_string::parse_string(line));","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(_title_prompt_help_tag);","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc@show_specific_help(help_key());","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"],"fact_sha256":"76d64884b42f8fd8cdcd34e7815e254f28143e50fcae8fde631331418efe1cad","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"help:wiz-monster","lifecycle":"wizard-only","producer":[{"anchor":"crawl-ref/source/wiz-mon.cc@\"wiz-monster\"#1","guards":["WIZARD"],"path":"crawl-ref/source/wiz-mon.cc"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"<h>生成指定怪物</h>\n\n要生成带有其物种默认装备的怪物，只需输入物种名称，\n例如<w>kobold</w>或<w>Yiuf</w>。\n\n可以把hydra或slymdra生成为指定头数，分别写作\nn<w>-headed</w> <w>hydra</w>或n<w>-headed</w> <w>slymdra</w>（头数为1至20）：\n\n<w>four-headed hydra</w>\n\n要设置（极）丑物刚生成时的颜色，可使用：\n\n<w>white very ugly thing</w>\n\n有效颜色为<w>brown</w>、<w>cyan</w>、<w>green</w>、<w>purple</w>、\n<w>red</w>和<w>white</w>。\n\n要定制变异兽，可在其名称前加上成熟度和/或一组以连字符分隔的特征：\n\n<w>mature shock-weird mutant beast</w>\n\n有效成熟度为<w>larval</w>、<w>juvenile</w>、<w>mature</w>、\n<w>elder</w>和<w>primal</w>。特征为<w>sting</w>、<w>bat</w>、<w>fire</w>、\n<w>weird</w>、<w>shock</w>和<w>ox</w>。\n\n要让黏液生物使用非正常体型，可加上前缀<w>large</w>、\n<w>very large</w>、<w>enormous</w>或<w>titanic</w>：\n\n<w>very large slime creature</w>\n\n如果怪物属于某种怪物群，可以在名称中加入关键词<w>band</w>，\n请求让它有资格带上同群成员。例如：\n\n<w>orc warlord band</w>\n\n指定<w>band</w>不会强制放置怪物群，只会让游戏按正常概率尝试生成。\n只有空间足够时才会放置怪物群。\n\n要给怪物指定物品，可在怪物名称后加分号，再接物品列表。例如：\n\n<w>orc ; rapier | quick blade . chain mail | plate armour</w>\n\n这会生成一个持握刺剑或迅捷之刃、穿戴锁子甲或板甲的兽人。\n只有明确请求时才会生成随机神器或附魔装备。\n请注意，怪物最初生成时携带的所有物品都会被移除并销毁。\n也可以借此强制怪物完全不携带物品：\n\n<w>orc; nothing</w>\n\n更多信息请参阅<cyan>docs/develop/levels/syntax.txt</cyan>中的\n<cyan>ITEMS</cyan>一节。这里的斜线（<w>/</w>）都应替换为竖线（<w>|</w>），\n逗号（<w>,</w>）都应替换为句点（<w>.</w>）。\n\n同一语法也可用于生成特定类型的舞动武器：\n\n<w>dancing weapon; rapier</w>\n\n限制：如果给怪物随机生成发射武器，就无法强制弹药类型与发射武器匹配。\n但有一个特例：舞动武器若为发射武器，总会同时生成一些合适的弹药。\n\n使用标签<w>always_corpse</w>可指定怪物必定生成尸体，\n使用<w>never_corpse</w>则可指定它绝不生成尸体。\n\n怪物默认在睡眠状态下生成。在名称前加上<w>generate_awake</w>可改变这一点。\n\n<w>generate_awake boulder beetle</w>\n\n前缀<w>patrolling</w>会阻止怪物走出其生成地点的视野范围。\n\n可以用<w>god</w>:说明符设置怪物的神祇：\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one</w>\n\n如果该怪物应当是指定神祇赐予的礼物，还需在其后加上<w>god_gift</w>：\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one god_gift</w>\n\n请注意，只有明确指定<w>god_gift</w>，怪物才会被视为神祇赠礼。\n\n可以用<w>att</w>:指定怪物的阵营，取值如下：\n\n* <w>att:hostile</w> -- 默认\n* <w>att:friendly</w> -- 驯服，会跟随你\n* <w>att:neutral</w> -- 对你和att:hostile怪物都抱有敌意\n* <w>att:good_neutral</w> -- 对att:hostile怪物抱有敌意\n* <w>att:fellow_slime</w> -- 驯服，但不会跟随你\n\n可以给怪物指定颜色，覆盖其默认颜色：\n\n<w>col:darkgrey fungus</w>\n\n也可以指定颜色<w>any</w>，此时会随机选择颜色。\n\n可以用<w>tile</w>:说明符覆盖怪物显示的贴图：\n\n<w>rat tile:mons_giant_bat</w>\n\n在贴图版中，这会生成一个使用“<cyan>mons_giant_bat</cyan>”贴图、\n其他方面与老鼠完全相同的怪物。在ASCII模式中，这项设置不起作用。\n\n要放置适合当前地图生成楼层的随机怪物，可以使用：\n\n<w>random</w>\n\n要放置适合其他地点的随机怪物，可以在怪物规格中使用place:标签：\n\n<w>place:Abyss</w>\n\n使用<w>place</w>:即表示要生成随机怪物。\n也可以请求把适合其他深度的随机怪物生成为亡灵，例如：\n\n<w>place:Elf:3 zombie</w>\n\n可用修饰语为<w>zombie</w>、<w>draugr</w>、<w>simulacrum</w>和<w>spectre</w>。\n\n<h>覆盖怪物名称</h>\n\n可以按如下方式给单个怪物命名：\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent</w>\n\n这会让怪物显示为“<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>”。\n名称中的空格可以用_符号代替。\n值得注意的是，“<cyan>the <<race></cyan>”将被附加到所有名称上，\n因此构思名称时应考虑这一点。\n\n名称可以使用三种不同的修饰标签：<w>name_adjective</w>、\n<w>name_suffix</w>和<w>name_replace</w>。<w>name_adjective</w>会让名称充当形容词。例如：\n\n<w>kobold name:ugly name_adjective</w>\n\n会产生“<cyan>An ugly kobold</cyan>”“<cyan>The ugly kobold hits you</cyan>”等文本。\n\n<w>name_suffix</w>的作用相同，但名称会放在怪物基础名称之后：\n\n<w>kobold name:wearing_mittens name_suffix</w>\n\n会产生“<cyan>A kobold wearing mittens</cyan>”\n“<cyan>The kobold wearing mittens hits you</cyan>”等文本。\n\n<w>name_replace</w>会用给定名称替换基础名称，如同该怪物是独有怪物：\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent name_replace</w>\n\n结果是“<cyan>Durwent</cyan>”而不是“<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>”，\n但尸体仍会显示为“<cyan>kobold corpse of Durwent</cyan>”。\n\n<w>name_species</w>会让尸体原样使用该名称；与<w>name_replace</w>一起使用时，\n不会在尸体名称前加上“<cyan>kobold</cyan> <cyan>corpse of X</cyan>”。\n在本地贴图版中，它还会避免在独有怪物和改名怪物上方显示通常的名称标签。\n\n与<w>name_replace</w>或<w>name_species</w>组合使用时，还有两个标签可用于\n重命名怪物、但不赋予怪物实际的“名字”：<w>name_descriptor</w>和<w>name_definite</w>。\n\n例如：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace</w>\n\n这会产生“<cyan>goblin hits you</cyan>”。但加入<w>name_descriptor</w>后，\n会产生“<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>”，怪物描述则会显示为\n“<cyan>A goblin</cyan>”。\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor</w>\n\n<w>name_definite</w>标签确保怪物绝不会使用“<cyan>a</cyan>”或\n“<cyan>an</cyan>”，而是始终使用定冠词。因此会显示\n“<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>”，怪物描述也会显示为\n“<cyan>The goblin</cyan>”。设置<w>name_definite</w>会自动设置\n<w>name_descriptor</w>，所以：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_definite</w>\n\n完全等同于：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor name_definite</w>\n\n<w>name_zombie</w>可用于重新定义已经亡灵化的怪物\n（包括draugr（尸鬼）、拟像和幽魂）。\n\n最后，<w>name_nocorpse</w>会让尸体完全忽略该名称。例如：\n\n<w>wizard name:sad name_adjective name_nocorpse</w>\n\n这会让“<cyan>a sad wizard</cyan>”留下“<cyan>a wizard corpse</cyan>”。\n\n如果定义中的空间有限，可以使用这些标签的缩写：\n\n* <w>name_suffix</w> -> <w>n_suf</w>\n* <w>name_adjective</w> -> <w>n_adj</w>\n* <w>name_replace</w> -> <w>n_rpl</w>\n* <w>name_species</w> -> <w>n_spe</w>\n* <w>name_descriptor</w> -> <w>n_des</w>\n* <w>name_definite</w> -> <w>n_the</w>\n* <w>name_zombie</w> -> <w>n_zom</w>\n* <w>name_nocorpse</w> -> <w>n_noc</w>\n\n<h>覆盖怪物属性</h>\n\n可以用<w>hd</w>和<w>hp</w>进一步定制怪物。\n这两个标签能完全覆盖怪物原有的生命骰和生命值：\n\n<w>kobold hd:20</w>\n\n这会生成一个生命骰为20的狗头人。它的HP会根据生命骰随机生成，\n因此修改HD后也会按新的HD重新掷出HP。显式指定了HP的怪物，其HP不会因此改变。\n\n也可以显式指定生命值：\n\n<w>kobold hp:20</w>\n\n这会生成一个生命值为20的狗头人。\n这对生命值原本不随机的怪物（如雕像）也很有用。\n\n可以用<w>dur</w>和<w>sum</w>标签把怪物标记为召唤物。\n<w>dur</w>标签接受1到6（含）之间的整数。<w>sum</w>标签接受法术名称\n（按下文所述的<w>spells</w>标签解析），也接受以下任一值：\n<w>clone</w>（Rakshasa，罗刹）、<w>animate</w>（来自“操纵死尸”）、\n<w>chaos</w>（由纯粹混沌生成）、<w>miscast</w>（由施法失误效果召唤）、\n<w>zot</w>（由佐特陷阱召唤）、<w>wrath</w>（神祇降下惩罚时召唤）、\n<w>aid</w>（神祇提供援助时召唤）。\n\n在法术中，<w>shadow_creatures</w>（暗影生物）的影响最明显：\n怪物会消散在暗影中，而不是化作一团烟雾消失。使用非召唤法术则不会产生效果。\n\n给怪物加上<w>seen</w>标签会覆盖系统设置，强制把它标记为已经见过；\n因此不会产生“<cyan>A kobold comes into view</cyan>”之类的消息。\n\n<h>覆盖怪物法术</h>\n\n可以用<w>spells</w>:标签覆盖怪物的法术集：\n\n*<w>goblin spells:throw_flame.50.wizard</w>\n*<w>ancient lich spells:symbol_of_torment.26.wizard; glaciate.51.wizard</w>\n\n（这是法术槽列表。每个槽由法术名称、施法频率和随后指定的法术槽标志组成；\n法术名称中的空格用下划线替换，各部分以<w>.</w>分隔，法术槽以<w>;</w>分隔。\n<w>;</w>两侧以及<w>spells</w>:前缀之后都不能有空格。）\n\n法术名称必须与<cyan>spl-data.h</cyan>中的名称完全一致，空格用下划线替换。\n法术频率表示施放该法术的概率（x/200）。法术标志包括：\n\n* <w>natural</w>、<w>magical</w>、<w>vocal</w>、<w>wizard</w>或\n<w>priest</w> —— 施放法术的方式。wizard、priest和vocal法术受沉默影响；\nwizard和priest法术会触发相应的\n神祇行为判定（特洛格会赞赏杀死巫师，比欧弗会赞赏杀死祭司）。\nmagical和wizard法术受禁魔效果影响。\nnatural和magical法术默认无声，除非标有noisy。\n* <w>emergency</w> —— 怪物只会在生命值较低时施放该法术。\n* <w>breath</w> —— 该法术是吐息武器；使用后会触发吐息冷却，\n冷却期间不能再次施放该法术。\n* <w>short_range</w> —— 仅在目标距离较近时施放该法术。\n* <w>long_range</w> —— 仅在目标距离较远时施放该法术。\n* <w>instant</w> —— 该法术不消耗怪物的能量；怪物可在同一回合采取另一个行动。\n* <w>noisy</w> —— 强制该法术发出声响，即使它属于默认无声的类型。\n\n可以用<w>none</w>指定法术槽为空。要强制生成无法术怪物，可使用：\n\n<w>orc wizard spells:none</w>\n\n<h>设置附魔效果</h>\n\n可以给怪物添加任意数量的附魔效果。怪物可以在生成时处于加速、失明状态，\n具有永久 RMsl 等。语法为<w>ench:<<name>:<<duration>:<<degree></w>或\n<w>perm_ench:<<name>:<<degree></w>。\n<w><<degree></w>和<w><<duration></w>字段均可省略。\n\n例如：\n\n* <w>orc perm_ench:blind perm_ench:mad</w>\n* <w>dream sheep ench:sticky_flame</w>\n* <w>stone giant dur:1 ench:berserk</w>\n","chinese_key_line":301,"chinese_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":5,"bullet_line_count":23,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</cyan>":25,"</h>":5,"</w>":157,"<cyan>":25,"<h>":5,"<w>":157},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{"<<degree>":3,"<<duration>":2,"<<name>":2,"<<race>":1},"technical_angle_sequence":["<<race>","<<name>","<<duration>","<<degree>","<<name>","<<degree>","<<degree>","<<duration>"]},"numbers":["1","20","3","20","20","20","20","1","6","50","26","51","200","1"],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":["docs/develop/levels/","x/"],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["kobold","Yiuf","-headed","hydra","-headed","slymdra","four-headed help = _query_database(HelpDB, topic, false, true);"},"dependency_group":"macros, platform keys, and wizard","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","english":"<h>Create specified monster</h>\n\nTo create a monster with the default equipment for its species, simply enter\nthe species name, such as <w>kobold</w> or <w>Yiuf</w>.\n\nA hydra or slymdra can be given a specific number of heads by calling it an\nn<w>-headed</w> <w>hydra</w> or n<w>-headed</w> <w>slymdra</w> (with a maximum of 20 heads):\n\n<w>four-headed hydra</w>\n\nTo set the colour a (very) ugly thing takes when it is first created, use:\n\n<w>white very ugly thing</w>\n\nThe valid colours are <w>brown</w>, <w>cyan</w>, <w>green</w>, <w>purple</w>, <w>red</w> and <w>white</w>.\n\nA mutant beast can be customised by prefacing it with a maturity level and/or\nhyphen-separated set of traits, as follows:\n\n<w>mature shock-weird mutant beast</w>\n\nThe valid maturity levels are <w>larval</w>, <w>juvenile</w>, <w>mature</w>, <w>elder</w> and <w>primal</w>. The\nfacets are <w>sting</w>, <w>bat</w>, <w>fire</w>, <w>weird</w>, <w>shock</w> and <w>ox</w>.\n\nA slime creature's size can be set to other than normal using the prefixes\n<w>large</w>, <w>very large</w>, <w>enormous</w> or <w>titanic</w>:\n\n<w>very large slime creature</w>\n\nIf a monster is a member of a band, you can request that it be eligible for\nband members by adding the keyword <w>band</w> to the name. For instance:\n\n<w>orc warlord band</w>\n\nSpecifying <w>band</w> doesn't force bands to be placed - it requests that the game\nuse the normal chances of creating a band. A band will only be placed if there\nis space for it.\n\nA monster can be given specific items by following the monster name with a\nsemi-colon and then with an item list. For example:\n\n<w>orc ; rapier | quick blade . chain mail | plate armour</w>\n\nwill generate an orc wielding either a rapier or a quick blade and wearing\neither chain mail or plate armour. Randarts and ego items are only generated\nif they are explicitly requested. Note that any items that the monster was\noriginally generated with will be removed and destroyed. This can be used to\nforce a monster to have no items whatsoever:\n\n<w>orc; nothing</w>\n\nFor further information, see <cyan>docs/develop/levels/syntax.txt</cyan>, section <cyan>ITEMS</cyan>.\nAny reference to a slash (<w>/</w>) should be replaced with a pipe (<w>|</w>) and any comma\n(<w>,</w>) with a full stop (<w>.</w>).\n\nThe same syntax is also used to create a specific type of dancing weapon:\n\n<w>dancing weapon; rapier</w>\n\nLimitations: If a monster is given a random launcher, there is no way to force\nthe ammo type to match the launcher type. As a special case, however, a\nlauncher dancing weapon is always generated with some suitable ammunition.\n\nYou can specify that a monster will always generate a corpse by using the tag\n<w>always_corpse</w>, or that it will never generate a corpse with <w>never_corpse</w>.\n\nBy default, monsters are generated asleep. This is changed by prefixing the\nname with <w>generate_awake</w>.\n\n<w>generate_awake boulder beetle</w>\n\nA prefix of <w>patrolling</w> prevents it from walking out of sight of the place\nwhere it was created.\n\nYou can set the monster's god using the <w>god</w>: specifier as follows:\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one</w>\n\nIf the monster is to be a gift of the specified god, this should be suffixed\nwith <w>god_gift</w>:\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one god_gift</w>\n\nNote that the monster will not be considered a god gift unless <w>god_gift</w> is\nexplicitly set.\n\nYou can set the monster's faction by specifying <w>att</w>: one of:\n\n* <w>att:hostile</w> -- the default\n* <w>att:friendly</w> -- tame, will follow you\n* <w>att:neutral</w> -- hostile to both you and att:hostile monsters\n* <w>att:good_neutral</w> -- hostile to att:hostile\n* <w>att:fellow_slime</w> -- tame, won't follow you\n\nMonsters can be given colours that override their default colour:\n\n<w>col:darkgrey fungus</w>\n\nThe colour <w>any</w> can be given, in which case a random colour will be chosen.\n\nYou can override the displayed monster tile using the <w>tile</w>: specifier as\nfollows:\n\n<w>rat tile:mons_giant_bat</w>\n\nIn tiles version, this would create a monster that uses the \"<cyan>mons_giant_bat</cyan>\"\ntile, but is otherwise identical to a rat. In ASCII mode, this will do\nnothing.\n\nIf you want to place a random monster suitable for the level the map is\ngenerated on, you can use\n\n<w>random</w>\n\nIf you want to place a random monster suitable for some other place, you can\nuse a place: tag in the monster spec:\n\n<w>place:Abyss</w>\n\nUsing <w>place</w>: implies that you want a random monster. You can also request\nzombies from random monsters suitable for some other depth as (e.g.):\n\n<w>place:Elf:3 zombie</w>\n\nThe available modifiers are <w>zombie</w>, <w>draugr</w>, <w>simulacrum</w>, and <w>spectre</w>.\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Names</h>\n\nIndividual monsters can be given names as follows:\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent</w>\n\nThis will cause the monster to appear as \"<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>\". Spaces can be\nplaced in the name by substituting them with the _ symbol. It is worth noting\nthat \"<cyan>the <<race></cyan>\" will be appended to all names, which should be considered\nwhen coming up with them.\n\nThere are three different modifiers that can be used on a name:\n<w>name_adjective</w>, <w>name_suffix</w> and <w>name_replace</w>. <w>name_adjective</w> makes the\nname act as an adjective. For example:\n\n<w>kobold name:ugly name_adjective</w>\n\nWill cause \"<cyan>An ugly kobold</cyan>\", \"<cyan>The ugly kobold hits you</cyan>\", and so on.\n\n<w>name_suffix</w> does the same, but after the monster's base name:\n\n<w>kobold name:wearing_mittens name_suffix</w>\n\nWill give \"<cyan>A kobold wearing mittens</cyan>\", \"<cyan>The kobold wearing mittens hits you</cyan>\",\nand so on.\n\n<w>name_replace</w> causes the base name to be replaced by given name, as if the\nmonster was a unique:\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent name_replace</w>\n\nWill result in \"<cyan>Durwent</cyan>\" rather than \"<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>\". The corpse will\nstill be a \"<cyan>kobold corpse of Durwent</cyan>\".\n\n<w>name_species</w> uses the name for corpses as-is, without prefixing it with\n\"<cyan>kobold</cyan> <cyan>corpse of X</cyan>\" for <w>name_replace</w>. It also avoids the usual name tag\nthat displays above unique and renamed monsters in local tiles.\n\nIn combination with <w>name_replace</w> or <w>name_species</w>, there are two further tags\nthat can be used when renaming monsters, but not giving them an actual \"name\":\n<w>name_descriptor</w>, and <w>name_definite</w>.\n\nFor example:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace</w>\n\nWill result in \"<cyan>goblin hits you</cyan>\". However, adding <w>name_descriptor</w> from the\nabove flags will result in \"<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>\" and in the description of the\nmonster, \"<cyan>A goblin</cyan>\".\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor</w>\n\nThe <w>name_definite</w> flag ensures that it is never referred to as\n\"<cyan>a</cyan>\" or \"<cyan>an</cyan>\", but that the definite article is always\nused. Therefore, you will see \"<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>\", as per above,\nbut also \"<cyan>The goblin</cyan>\" in its description.\nSetting <w>name_definite</w> will automatically set the flag <w>name_descriptor</w>,\ntherefore meaning that:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_definite</w>\n\nis exactly the same as:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor name_definite</w>\n\n<w>name_zombie</w> can be used to redefine already zombified monsters (including\ndraugr, simulacra and spectrals).\n\nFinally, <w>name_nocorpse</w> will cause the name to be completely ignored for\ncorpses. For example:\n\n<w>wizard name:sad name_adjective name_nocorpse</w>\n\nwill result in \"<cyan>a sad wizard</cyan>\" that leaves \"<cyan>a wizard corpse</cyan>\".\n\nFor instances where space in definitions is limited, you can use abbreviated\nforms of these tags:\n\n* <w>name_suffix</w> -> <w>n_suf</w>\n* <w>name_adjective</w> -> <w>n_adj</w>\n* <w>name_replace</w> -> <w>n_rpl</w>\n* <w>name_species</w> -> <w>n_spe</w>\n* <w>name_descriptor</w> -> <w>n_des</w>\n* <w>name_definite</w> -> <w>n_the</w>\n* <w>name_zombie</w> -> <w>n_zom</w>\n* <w>name_nocorpse</w> -> <w>n_noc</w>\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Stats</h>\n\nFurther monster customisation can be made by using <w>hd</w> and <w>hp</w>. These two tags\nallow you to completely overwrite the original hit dice and hit points of a\nmonster:\n\n<w>kobold hd:20</w>\n\nCreates a kobold with a hit dice of 20. As its hp is randomised, based on its\nhit dice, this will also be re-rolled. Monsters with a specific hit point\nvalue will not have this value change.\n\nYou can also have a specific value for hit points:\n\n<w>kobold hp:20</w>\n\nCreates a kobold with 20 hit points. This is also useful for monsters (such as\nstatues) which come with non-randomised HP values.\n\nMonsters can be marked as summoned by using <w>dur</w> and <w>sum</w> tags. The <w>dur</w> tag\naccepts an integer between 1 and 6 inclusive. The <w>sum</w> tag accepts the name of\na spell (parsed as per the <w>spells</w> tag, described below) or any of <w>clone</w>\n(Rakshasa), <w>animate</w> (from animate dead), <w>chaos</w> (made from pure chaos), <w>miscast</w>\n(summoned from by spell miscast effect), <w>zot</w> (summoned by a Zot trap), <w>wrath</w>\n(summoned by a god in an act of retribution), <w>aid</w> (summoned by a god in order\nto give aid).\n\nOf the spells, <w>shadow_creatures</w> has the most significant effect, in that\nmonsters will dissipate into the shadows rather than disappear in a puff of\nsmoke. Using non-summoning spells will also have no effect.\n\nTagging a monster with <w>seen</w> will override the system and force that monster to\nbe marked as already viewed; this means that it won't generate messages such\nas \"<cyan>A kobold comes into view</cyan>\".\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Spells</h>\n\nMonster spell sets can be overridden with a <w>spells</w>: tag, used as follows:\n\n*<w>goblin spells:throw_flame.50.wizard</w>\n*<w>ancient lich spells:symbol_of_torment.26.wizard; glaciate.51.wizard</w>\n\n(a list of spell slots, consisting of a spell name with spaces replaced with\nunderscores, a frequency of casting and set of spell slot flags specified\nafterwards with <w>.</w>, with slots separated by <w>;</w>. There should be no spaces around\nthe <w>;</w> or after the <w>spells</w>: prefix)\n\nSpell names must exactly match the names in <cyan>spl-data.h</cyan>, with spaces replaced\nby underscores. Spell frequencies are an (x in 200) chance that that spell\nwill be cast. Spell flags include:\n\n* <w>natural</w>, <w>magical</w>, <w>vocal</w>, <w>wizard</w>, or <w>priest</w> - the manner in which this\nspell is being cast. Wizard, priest, and vocal spells are affected by silence.\nWizards and priests trigger appropriate conducts (Trog will appreciate killing\nwizards, Beogh will appreciate killing priests). Magical and wizard spells are\nsubject to antimagic effects. Natural and magical spells do not generate noise\nunless marked <w>noisy</w>.\n* <w>emergency</w> - the monster will only cast the spell when low on health.\n* <w>breath</w> - the spell is a breath weapon, and using it triggers a breath\ntimeout during which the spell cannot be cast again.\n* <w>instant</w> - the spell does not expend the monster's energy; they may take\nanother action on the same turn.\n* <w>noisy</w> - forces the spell to make noise even if it is a natural or magical\nability.\n* <w>short_range</w> - the monster will only cast the spell when its target is at\nshort range.\n* <w>long_range</w> - the monster will only cast the spell when its target is at\nlong range.\n\nYou may use <w>none</w> to specify that a slot should be left empty. You can force a\nspell-less monster with:\n\n<w>orc wizard spells:none</w>\n\n<h>Setting enchantments</h>\n\nYou may give a monster any number of enchantments. It may start hasted, blind,\nhave permanent RMsl, etc. The syntax is <w>ench:<<name>:<<duration>:<<degree></w> or\n<w>perm_ench:<<name>:<<degree></w>. The <w><<degree></w> and <w><<duration></w> fields may be\nomitted.\n\nFor example:\n\n* <w>orc perm_ench:blind perm_ench:mad</w>\n* <w>dream sheep ench:sticky_flame</w>\n* <w>stone giant dur:1 ench:berserk</w>\n","english_key_line":312,"english_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":5,"bullet_line_count":23,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</cyan>":25,"</h>":5,"</w>":158,"<cyan>":25,"<h>":5,"<w>":158},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<cyan>","</cyan>","<w>", {"actual_behavior":"胜利目标是取得佐特宝珠并返回地面，进入佐特领域前需从不同分支收集三个符文；新手可先尝试多种角色。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 显然是生存并获胜。为此，你需要深入地牢，找到传说中的佐特宝珠，\n并将其带回地面。要进入佐特领域，首先需要收集散落在地牢各个分支中的三个符文。\n\n不过，如果你刚接触这个游戏，实在不必为此担心。只管尝试尽可能深入地前进，\n并用不同种族、技能组合和信仰的各种角色探索Crawl众多支线，享受其中的乐趣。","question":"这个游戏的目标到底是什么？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: To survive and to win, obviously. For this you need to find the fabled Orb\nof Zot deep down in the dungeon and bring it back to the surface. To enter the\nrealm of Zot, you'll first need to collect three runes to be found in various\nbranches of the dungeon.\n\nHowever, if you're new to the game, you really shouldn't worry about this. Just\ntry to delve as deeply as possible and have fun exploring the many side\nbranches in Crawl with various characters of different species, skill sets, and\nreligions.","question":"What exactly is the goal of this game?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; {"actual_behavior":"本地游戏的 chardump 与结算高分会指出 morgue 位置，saves 与 morgue 同级，morgue_dir/save_dir 可分别配置。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"如果你在本地游玩（即不在服务器上），chardump命令会告诉你\nmorgue文件写入的位置；游戏结束时，高分记录下方也会显示这一信息。\n\nsaves/目录与morgue/目录位于同一目录下。\n\n如果想更改任一位置，请在init文件中修改morgue_dir和/或save_dir选项。","question":"我的存档、morgue和角色转储文件在哪里？"},"current_english":{"answer":"If you're playing locally (i.e. not on one of the servers), the chardump\ncommand will tell you where the morgue file is written, and this information is\nalso displayed below the highscore at the end of a game.\n\nThe saves/ directory can be found in the same directory as the morgue/ one.\n\nIf you'd like to change either location, modify the morgue_dir and/or save_dir\noptions in the init file.","question":"I can't find my save files, morgue and character dumps. {"actual_behavior":"条目列出种族/背景权重、骨骸文件、不可出售、抗性来源、可学习获胜，以及 o/G/X/Ctrl-F/Tab 等界面命令。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 首先，你或许应该阅读手册中的设计理念章节（'?N'），\n了解Stone Soup游戏设计的一般重要原则。\n\n至于具体差异，最重要的可能是以下几点：\n\n* 种族比角色背景（或“职业”）重要*得多*。\n\n* 骨骸文件只包含鬼魂，绝不包含其装备或死因。\n\n* 你不能向商店或其他任何地方出售物品。\n\n* 抗性可以通过变异或物品获得，绝不能通过进食获得。\n\n* 死亡是难免的，但新手也能在不看攻略的情况下取胜，\n  只要能从错误中吸取教训。非常优秀的玩家可以连续赢下好几局。\n\n此外，我们致力于为Crawl提供非常流畅的界面，所以你确实应该找时间\n通读命令列表（'??'）。以下命令尤其方便：\n\n* 'o'，自动探索未知楼层\n\n* 'G'，自动前往另一楼层（用'G?'查看帮助）\n\n* 'X'，打开楼层地图，以便旅行及执行其他任务（'X?'）\n\n* 'Ctrl-F'，查找并前往以前见过的物品（'Ctrl-F?'）\n\n* 'Tab'，移向或攻击最近的怪物","question":"与其他流行的roguelike游戏相比，最大的区别是什么？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: First, you might want to read the philosophy section in the manual ('?N') to\nread about general important principles in Stone Soup's game design.\n\nAs for specific differences, the most important are probably the following:\n\n* Species is *much* more important than the character background (or \"class\").\n\n* Bones files only contain ghosts, never their equipment or cause of death.\n\n* You cannot sell items to shops or elsewhere.\n\n* Resistances can be gained via mutations or items, never through eating.\n\n* There are inevitable deaths, but new players can win without spoilers, if\n  they learn from their mistakes. Very good players can win several consecutive\n  games.\n\nAlso, our aim is to give Crawl a really smooth interface, so you should really\nread through the command list ('??') some time. The following commands are\nparticularly convenient:\n\n* 'o', for automatic exploration of an unknown level\n\n* 'G', for automatic travel to another level ('G?' for help)\n\n* 'X', to access the level map for travelling and other tasks ('X?')\n\n* 'Ctrl-F', to find and travel to items you've seen before ('Ctrl-F?')\n\n* 'Tab', to move towards or attack the nearest monster","question":"What are the biggest differences to other popular roguelike games?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt@q:roguelike {"actual_behavior":"新手可用提示模式、选择较容易的组合、谨慎处理新怪物并关注 HP/MP；`#` 生成角色转储以供社区建议。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 如果你连神殿都很难到达，可以尝试用提示模式玩一两局，\n获取一些通用的游戏提示。\n\n此外，有些角色组合较简单，有些则更具挑战性（在选择界面中以深灰色标记）。\n如果你刚接触Crawl，建议坚持选择较简单的组合。通常应当极其谨慎地对待\n遇到的每种新怪物，尽量选择与怪物交战的地点，并随时留意生命值和法力。\n\n如果需要针对特定角色的具体建议，可以在游戏中按'#'，\n在morgue目录中创建一份详细列出装备、物品、技能训练和可用法术的“角色转储文件”。\n你可以通过http://crawl.develz.org/列出的任一社区讨论渠道与其他玩家分享该文件，\n获取反馈和建议。\n","question":"怎样才能不总是死掉？能给我一些游戏技巧吗？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: If you've got trouble even reaching the Temple, you might want to try a game\nor two in hints mode for some general playing hints.\n\nAlso, there are easy playing combinations and more challenging ones (marked in\ndark grey on the selection screen). If you are new to Crawl, sticking to the\neasier ones is advisable. In general, treat each new monster you meet with\nutmost caution, try to choose the location where you fight monsters, and keep\nan eye on your health and magic.\n\nFor specific hints dealing with a particular character, you can press '#'\nin-game to create a \"character dump\" in your morgue directory that details your\nequipment, items, skilling, and available spells. You can share this with other\nplayers to get feedback and advice through any of the community discussion\nresources listed at http://crawl.develz.org/.\n","question":"How can I avoid dying all the time? {"actual_behavior":"缺失的下楼梯可能位于本层不可从当前上楼梯到达的区域，也可能已到分支底层；Ctrl-O 与 ?/b 提供分支信息。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 下楼梯可能位于本层某个区域，而从你来时的那座上楼梯无法到达该区域。\n试着返回上一层，再从另一组楼梯下到本层。请注意，大多数楼层都有\n三座下楼梯和三座上楼梯，逃生舱口不计在内。\n\n或者，如果你身处某个分支，也可能已经到达该分支的最底层，\n必须掉头去探索另一个分支。查看地牢概览（'Ctrl-O'）可了解你在指定分支中\n已经深入到哪一层；查看数据库（'?/b'）则可了解尚未见过的分支共有多少层。","question":"我找不到下楼梯。是不是漏掉了什么？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: They may be in a part of the level that is not reachable from the upstairs\nyou came from. Try going up to the previous level and coming back down from\nthere via a different set of stairs. Note that most levels feature three\ndownstairs as well as three upstairs, not counting any escape hatches.\n\nAlternatively, if you are in one of the branches, you might also have reached\nits lowest level and have to turn around to explore another one. Check the\ndungeon overview ('Ctrl-O') to see how deep you've delved into a given branch,\nor the database ('?/b') for the number of levels for unseen branches.","question":"I can't find any downstairs. {"actual_behavior":"武器比较取决于技能、单双手、随机神器属性、铭印和目标；描述或装备时按 `@` 可查看延迟与伤害评级。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: “更好”取决于许多因素，例如你的武器技能、武器是单手还是双手、\n随机神器属性、武器铭印，以及你即将面对的怪物。一般来说，\n从已经非常熟练的武器类型改用完全没有技能的另一类型是在浪费时间。\n不同铭印造成的伤害可能相差很大；你可以分别用两把武器敲打几个相对无害的怪物，\n比较结果，从而得到大致判断。\n\n武器的攻击延迟可以通过提升武器技能等级来降低。\n你可以阅读武器描述，或在装备该武器时按'@'，查看攻击延迟和伤害评级。\n赤手空拳时也可以按'@'。","question":"我有两把很酷的武器。怎么判断哪把更好？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: \"Better\" depends on a number of factors, such as your weapon skills, whether\nit's one- or two-handed, the randart properties, the weapon brand, and the\nmonsters you're about to face. In general, switching from a weapon type at\nwhich you are already highly skilled to another one that you have no skill in\nis a waste of time. The brands may greatly differ in damage output, but you can\nget a good idea by bashing a few comparatively harmless monsters with each and\ncomparing the results.\n\nThe delay in swinging a weapon can be reduced by the corresponding weapon skill.\nYou can view your delay and damage rating with a weapon by reading its\ndescription or pressing '@' while the weapon is equipped. '@' works while\nunarmed too.","question":"I've got two cool weapons. How can I tell which one is better?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; {"actual_behavior":"新手近战、远程和施法各有直观神祇选择；祭坛 `>` 阅读说明，信徒在 `^` 页面用 `!` 查看更详细描述。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 最直观易用的神祇大概是特洛格或奥卡瓦鲁（近战），\n惠普利亚卡纳或奥卡瓦鲁（远程战斗），维胡梅特或西芙·穆娜（施法）。\n此外，佐姆虽然不可靠，却可能非常有趣。\n\n每当遇到祭坛，都务必阅读对应神祇的描述（在祭坛上按'>'祈祷），\n并仔细考虑是否皈依该神祇。成为信徒后，可以在'^'页面重新阅读神祇描述，\n并用'!'切换显示更详细的说明。\n\n主地牢前十层保证能找到几乎所有神祇的祭坛。","question":"你会向新手推荐哪些神祇？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: The most straightforward gods to play are probably Trog or Okawaru (for melee\ncombat), Hepliaklqana or Okawaru (for ranged combat), Vehumet or Sif Muna (for\nspellcasting). Also, while unreliable, Xom can be a lot of fun.\n\nWhenever you encounter an altar, be sure to read the corresponding god's\ndescription (by praying on the altar with '>') and take your time in deciding\nwhether to pick this deity or not. As a follower, you can reread your god's\ndescription, now more detailed via the '!' toggle, on the '^' screen.\n\nYou're guaranteed to find altars to almost every god on the first ten levels of\nthe main dungeon.","question":"Which gods would you recommend to a beginning player?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt@q:religion","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt@a:religion","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt@q:religion","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt@a:religion","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"],"fact_sha256":"98c5f8274b583ef849a5079ac9d9f7b4640ba42876af9d40d4a93438b457981b","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"faq:religion","lifecycle":"current-faq-menu-entry","producer":{"chinese_question_order":7,"english_question_order":7,"source_note":"File order is authoring evidence; runtime menu order is DBM iteration order and is not guaranteed by the current consumer."},"production_facts":{"chinese":{"answer":"A: 最直观易用的神祇大概是特洛格或奥卡瓦鲁（近战），\n惠普利亚卡纳或奥卡瓦鲁（远程战斗），维胡梅特或西芙·穆娜（施法）。\n此外，佐姆虽然不可靠，却可能非常有趣。\n\n每当遇到祭坛，都务必阅读对应神祇的描述（在祭坛上按'>'祈祷），\n并仔细考虑是否皈依该神祇。成为信徒后，可以在'^'页面重新阅读神祇描述，\n并用'!'切换显示更详细的说明。\n\n主地牢前十层保证能找到几乎所有神祇的祭坛。","question":"你会向新手推荐哪些神祇？"},"chinese_key_lines":{"answer":157,"question":153},"chinese_tokens":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[">","^","!"],"urls":[],"w_contents":[]},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":[]}},"consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","english":{"answer":"A: The most straightforward gods to play are probably Trog or Okawaru (for melee\ncombat), Hepliaklqana or Okawaru (for ranged combat), Vehumet or Sif Muna (for\nspellcasting). Also, while unreliable, Xom can be a lot of fun.\n\nWhenever you encounter an altar, be sure to read the corresponding god's\ndescription (by praying on the altar with '>') and take your time in deciding\nwhether to pick this deity or not. As a follower, you can reread your god's\ndescription, now more detailed via the '!' toggle, on the '^' screen.\n\nYou're guaranteed to find altars to almost every god on the first ten levels of\nthe main dungeon.","question":"Which gods would you recommend to a beginning player?"},"english_key_lines":{"answer":180,"question":176},"english_tokens":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[">","!","^"],"urls":[],"w_contents":[]},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":[]}},"fact_sha256":"98c5f8274b583ef849a5079ac9d9f7b4640ba42876af9d40d4a93438b457981b","identity":"faq:religion","key":"religion","kind":"faq","lifecycle":"current-faq-menu-entry","producer":{"chinese_question_order":7,"english_question_order":7,"source_note":"File order is authoring evidence; {"actual_behavior":"玩家鬼魂不能关闭，但生成在封闭宝库中，玩家可选择是否打开；也可用强势组合清除或在线遇到他人鬼魂。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 没有这种选项，我们也不打算添加。\n\n请记住，你并非必须与遇到的每只怪物战斗；面对玩家鬼魂时更容易避战：\n它们会生成在封闭的宝库中，而是否打开每座鬼魂宝库由你决定。\n\n或者，你可以专门用几个强势的种族/背景组合角色消灭这些鬼魂，\n例如巨魔狂战士。也可以改玩在线版——那样你多半会被其他玩家的鬼魂杀死！","question":"我总是被自己的玩家鬼魂杀死。能禁用它们吗？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: There isn't any such option, and we're not planning to add one, either.\n\nKeep in mind that you don't absolutely have to fight every monster you\nmeet, and with ghosts it's even easier: they are generated in sealed\nvaults, and it is your choice whether to open each ghost vault.\n\nAlternatively, you could play a couple characters of strong species/background\ncombinations for the sole purpose of busting those ghosts, e.g. Troll\nBerserkers. Or play online, where you'll mostly be killed by others' ghosts\ninstead!","question":"I keep getting murdered by my own ghosts. {"actual_behavior":"逃离深渊依赖稀有出口、持续移动、防御装备、延迟传送、卢格努、深入层数和有选择地杀敌生成出口。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 如果这是你第一次进入深渊，逃出去的机会很小。不过仍有一些通用建议：\n\n* 你需要找到通往外界的传送门。这种传送门很罕见！\n\n* 不要停下来战斗；尽可能多地移动！\n\n* 如果有防御性装备，就用上它们（防护铭印武器等）。\n\n* 使用传送，前往深渊中完全不同的区域。（但请记住，\n  在深渊中，传送要比在地牢中延迟更久才会生效……）\n\n* 考虑转而皈依深渊之神卢格努。一旦皈依，你就能立刻逃出深渊\n  （但可能会激怒你先前信奉的神祇！）。\n\n* 进入深渊的更深层会提高找到出口的概率，但也会增加你面临的危险。\n\n* 杀死敌人可能会生成通往外界的传送门，但大多数敌人都太危险，不适合交战；\n  请谨慎选择战斗！","question":"我不知怎么来到了一个叫“深渊”的地方。怎样才能离开？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: If you are in the Abyss for the first time, chances of getting out are\nslim. Still, here are a few general hints:\n\n* You need to find a gateway leading out of the Abyss. These are rare!\n\n* Don't stick around to fight; move as much as possible!\n\n* If you have it, use defensive equipment (weapons of protection, etc.).\n\n* Use teleportation to end up in a completely new part of the Abyss. (But keep\n  in mind that teleportation will take much longer to kick in than in the\n  Dungeon...)\n\n* Consider switching your allegiance to Lugonu, the god of the Abyss. Upon\n  conversion, you will be able to escape the Abyss immediately (but you may\n  anger your previous god!).\n\n* Descending deeper in the Abyss will increase the chance of finding an exit\n  out, but also increase the danger you face.\n\n* Killing enemies can create a gateway out, but most enemies will be\n  too dangerous to fight; pick your battles carefully!","question":"I've somehow ended up in a place called \"Abyss\". How do I get out of here?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt@q:abyss","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt@a:abyss","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt@q:abyss","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt@a:abyss","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"],"fact_sha256":"0b84506c8e6412d3aca593601ee76be476e3190ed353b70f3df7ee68e6911a4b","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@366e807eaae5403b6c3925df5970cd237b447ead76fdb717b71273473b5db67e","identity":"faq:abyss","lifecycle":"current-faq-menu-entry","producer":{"chinese_question_order":9,"english_question_order":9,"source_note":"File order is authoring evidence; These are rare!\n\n* Don't stick around to fight; move as much as possible!\n\n* If you have it, use defensive equipment (weapons of protection, etc.).\n\n* Use teleportation to end up in a completely new part of the Abyss. (But keep\n  in mind that teleportation will take much longer to kick in than in the\n  Dungeon...)\n\n* Consider switching your allegiance to Lugonu, the god of the Abyss. Upon\n  conversion, you will be able to escape the Abyss immediately (but you may\n  anger your previous god!).\n\n* Descending deeper in the Abyss will increase the chance of finding an exit\n  out, but also increase the danger you face.\n\n* Killing enemies can create a gateway out, but most enemies will be\n  too dangerous to fight; pick your battles carefully!","question":"I've somehow ended up in a place called \"Abyss\". {"actual_behavior":"randart 是随机神器，unrandart 是预定义神器；神器不能重新铭印，通常不能附魔，但神器附魔变异只允许随机神器武器/护甲强化到普通同类上限。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 除了散落在地牢中的普通物品，你还可能遇到神器。神器分为两类：\n\n* randart：随机神器，通常是武器、护甲或饰品，具有随机属性，\n  例如提供抗性或能力。\n\n* unrandart：较罕见的非随机神器，其属性预先定义，而非随机生成。\n  大多数非随机神器一眼就能鉴定出来，只有一部分带有其他地方无法获得的\n  特殊效果或独特能力。\n\n所有神器都不能被暂时或永久地重新铭印。神器通常也不能附魔；\n但拥有“神器附魔”变异时，可以用武器附魔卷轴或护甲附魔卷轴\n强化随机神器武器或随机神器护甲，且不能超过同类普通物品的强化上限。\n非随机神器即使拥有该变异也不能附魔。","question":"术语“randart”和“unrandart”分别是什么意思？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: Apart from the standard items you'll find lying around in the dungeon, you\nmight also stumble upon artefacts which come in two varieties:\n\n* randart: a random artefact, usually a weapon, armour or jewellery, with\n  random properties such as conferring resistances or abilities.\n\n* unrandart: a rarer variety of artefact that is predefined instead of randomly\n  generated. Most unrandarts are identified on sight, and some come with\n  special effects or unique abilities that are not available elsewhere.\n\nArtefacts cannot be rebranded. They also normally cannot be enchanted, but\nthe artefact enchanting mutation allows scrolls of enchant weapon and enchant\narmour to improve randart weapons and armour up to the normal enchantment\nlimits for mundane items of the same type. Unrandarts cannot be enchanted even\nwith this mutation.","question":"What do the terms \"randart\" and \"unrandart\" signify?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; {"actual_behavior":"Stone Soup 的 0.X 编号不表示不稳定；编号主要因历史与避免混淆而保留，未来是否更改并不确定。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 实际上，对于一款开发如此活跃的游戏，我们如今认为Stone Soup已经相当稳定，\n所以请不要纠结0.X版本号，尽管试一试。\n\n我们沿用这一版本编号，主要是出于历史原因，也因为改动它可能造成不必要的混乱。\n未来某个时候也许会改，也许不会。","question":"什么时候才会开发到“稳定”的1.0版本？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: Actually, by now we consider Stone Soup to be reasonably stable for a game\nof such active development, so please don't get hung up about the 0.X\nversioning and give it a try.\n\nWe've mostly kept the versioning for historic reasons and because it might\ncause undue confusion. We might decide to change that at some point, but maybe\nwe won't.","question":"When will development reach the \"stable\" version 1.0?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"project, release, support, and performance","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; {"actual_behavior":"下一版本可通过在线 trunk 或 Windows/Mac 二进制提前体验；trunk 可能有 bug，极少使存档失效，报告需附 `?v` 版本。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 当然有！你可以在任一在线服务器上游玩“trunk”\n（它最终会成为下一个版本）；https://crawl.develz.org\n还提供可下载的Windows和Mac二进制文件。请注意，trunk可能存在错误，\n而且极少数情况下，错误可能会使你的存档失效。如果你不介意，那就尽管玩吧！\n\n请在github上报告错误（https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues）。\n\n报告时请注明当前游玩的版本（见'?v'），\n因为该错误可能已经修复，或相关功能可能已经实现。","question":"新版本发布前，有办法先玩吗？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: There certainly is! You can play \"trunk\" (what will eventually become the\nnext version) online on any of the online servers; https://crawl.develz.org\nalso hosts Windows and Mac binaries you can download. Note that trunk may be\nbuggy, and that very rarely a bug may invalidate your saved games. If that\ndoesn't bother you, go ahead and play!\n\nPlease report bugs on github (https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues).\n\nWhen doing so, please mention the version (see '?v') you're currently playing,\nin case the bug's already been fixed or the feature already implemented.","question":"Is there a way for me to play the new version before it is released?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"project, release, support, and performance","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; {"actual_behavior":"GitHub bug 报告应包括操作、预期/实际结果、`?v` 版本、console/tiles；必要时提供存档、服务器/用户名、截图、错误消息和复现步骤。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 请在github上提交错误报告（https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues），\n并尽可能提供详细信息：\n\n* 那时你在做什么？\n\n* 你原本预期会发生什么，实际却发生了什么，等等。\n\n* 最重要的是，你正在玩哪个版本？\n  （如果不确定，可以在游戏中用'?v'查看。）\n\n* 控制台版还是贴图版？\n\n如果发生崩溃，或怪物/物品/楼层行为异常，我们可能需要存档文件。\n如果你玩的是在线控制台版，可以在版本菜单中选择正在游玩的版本，\n再按'A'选择Advanced options（高级选项），创建一份开发者可访问的存档副本。\n别忘了在错误报告中注明服务器和用户名！\n\n截图有时会很有帮助。如有错误消息，请把它们记录下来。\n如果无法提供存档，说明如何复现崩溃（或错误）的步骤也很有用；\n你可以使用巫师模式来复现。\n\n预先感谢！","question":"游戏总是崩溃。/我觉得发现了一个错误。该怎么办？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: Please submit a bug report on github (https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues),\nand give as much information as you can:\n\n* What were you doing at the time?\n\n* What did you expect to happen, what happened instead, etc.\n\n* Most importantly, what version are you playing?\n  (If you're not sure, you can check in-game with '?v'.)\n\n* Console or tiles?\n\nFor crashes or monsters/items/levels behaving oddly, we probably need a save\nfile. If you're playing online console, you can create a copy of your save file\nfor the developers to access by selecting the version you are playing and then\npressing 'A' for Advanced options. Don't forget to include the server and your\nusername in your bug report!\n\nSometimes a screenshot can be helpful. If there are error messages, please\nwrite them down. Short of a save file, a guide on how to reproduce the crash\n(or bug) can be useful; you can use wizard mode for this.\n\nThanks in advance!","question":"The game keeps crashing. / I think I found a bug. runtime menu order is DBM iteration order and is not guaranteed by the current consumer."},"production_facts":{"chinese":{"answer":"A: 请在github上提交错误报告（https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues），\n并尽可能提供详细信息：\n\n* 那时你在做什么？\n\n* 你原本预期会发生什么，实际却发生了什么，等等。\n\n* 最重要的是，你正在玩哪个版本？\n  （如果不确定，可以在游戏中用'?v'查看。）\n\n* 控制台版还是贴图版？\n\n如果发生崩溃，或怪物/物品/楼层行为异常，我们可能需要存档文件。\n如果你玩的是在线控制台版，可以在版本菜单中选择正在游玩的版本，\n再按'A'选择Advanced options（高级选项），创建一份开发者可访问的存档副本。\n别忘了在错误报告中注明服务器和用户名！\n\n截图有时会很有帮助。如有错误消息，请把它们记录下来。\n如果无法提供存档，说明如何复现崩溃（或错误）的步骤也很有用；\n你可以使用巫师模式来复现。\n\n预先感谢！","question":"游戏总是崩溃。/我觉得发现了一个错误。该怎么办？"},"chinese_key_lines":{"answer":275,"question":271},"chinese_tokens":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":4,"bullet_line_count":4,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":["?v","A"],"urls":["https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues"],"w_contents":[]},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":[]}},"consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"dependency_group":"project, release, support, and performance","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","english":{"answer":"A: Please submit a bug report on github (https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues),\nand give as much information as you can:\n\n* What were you doing at the time?\n\n* What did you expect to happen, what happened instead, etc.\n\n* Most importantly, what version are you playing?\n  (If you're not sure, you can check in-game with '?v'.)\n\n* Console or tiles?\n\nFor crashes or monsters/items/levels behaving oddly, we probably need a save\nfile. If you're playing online console, you can create a copy of your save file\nfor the developers to access by selecting the version you are playing and then\npressing 'A' for Advanced options. Don't forget to include the server and your\nusername in your bug report!\n\nSometimes a screenshot can be helpful. If there are error messages, please\nwrite them down. Short of a save file, a guide on how to reproduce the crash\n(or bug) can be useful; you can use wizard mode for this.\n\nThanks in advance!","question":"The game keeps crashing. / I think I found a bug.
