{"actual_behavior":"贡献范围包括地图/宝库、怪物台词、描述和贴图；补丁指南、提交入口及三类拒绝原因均需完整保留。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 有几个领域可以发挥创造力，我们非常感谢外部帮助：\n\n* 地图/宝库（见dat/目录和docs/develop/levels/introduction.txt）\n\n* 怪物台词（见dat/database/和docs/develop/monster_speech.txt）\n\n* 怪物/物品/法术描述（见dat/descript/）\n\n* 贴图（见source/rltiles/和docs/develop/tiles_creation.txt）\n\n此外，如果你想编写补丁却不知如何着手，请阅读docs/develop/目录中的patch_guide.txt。\n请在我们的github仓库提交补丁及其他新内容：\nhttps://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues\n\n请记住，如果事先没有讨论，我们可能无法采用该补丁，\n或需要进行大幅修改。补丁也可能不被接受，通常有以下三个原因：\n\n* 它不符合我们所理解的Crawl世界。\n\n* 它违背了Crawl避免重复刷取和高度依赖剧透的内容这一设计理念。\n\n* 我们认为它不够有趣，不值得实现。\n\n非常感谢你的支持！","question":"我想帮助改进游戏。能做些什么？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: There are several areas where you can get creative and outside help is much\nappreciated. These are:\n\n* maps/vaults (see dat/ directory, docs/develop/levels/introduction.txt)\n\n* monster speech (see dat/database/, docs/develop/monster_speech.txt)\n\n* monster/item/spell descriptions (see dat/descript/)\n\n* tiles (see source/rltiles/, docs/develop/tiles_creation.txt)\n\nAlso, if you'd like to write a patch but don't know how to go about it, read\npatch_guide.txt in the docs/develop/ directory. Submit it and other new things\nat our github repository: https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues\n\nKeep in mind that without previous discussions, we may not be able to use the\npatch, or it might need heavy modification. It's possible that it won't be\naccepted, usually for one of three reasons:\n\n* It doesn't fit into the Crawl world as we see it.\n\n* It violates Crawl's philosophy to avoid grinding and spoiler-heavy stuff.\n\n* We just don't think it's interesting enough to ever be implemented.\n\nThanks a lot for your support!","question":"I'd like to help improve the game. What can I do?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"project, release, support, and performance","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines creative and outside help is much\nappreciated. These are:\n\n* maps/vaults (see dat/ directory, docs/develop/levels/introduction.txt)\n\n* monster speech (see dat/database/, docs/develop/monster_speech.txt)\n\n* monster/item/spell descriptions (see dat/descript/)\n\n* tiles (see source/rltiles/, docs/develop/tiles_creation.txt)\n\nAlso, if you'd like to write a patch but don't know how to go about it, read\npatch_guide.txt in the docs/develop/ directory. Submit it and other new things\nat our github repository: https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues\n\nKeep in mind that without previous discussions, we may not be able to use the\npatch, or it might need heavy modification. {"actual_behavior":"贴图版依赖 OpenGL/SDL 与硬件加速；更新 Windows 驱动、启用 Linux DRI、调整 tiles_help 选项或改用控制台版均是现有建议。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@string val = unwrap_desc(_query_database(FAQDB, key, false, true));","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@val = replace_all(val, \"\\n\\n*\", \"\\n•\");","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc@return _database_find_keys(FAQDB.get(), \"^q.+\", false);","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@static void _handle_FAQ()","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc@string question = getFAQ_Question(question_keys[i]);"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: Crawl使用OpenGL和SDL渲染其图形。因为它用的是OpenGL，\n所以要依靠一点硬件加速才能使其迅速运行。如果你的系统未启用此功能，\n那么Crawl会运行得很差。\n\n在Windows上，Windows自带的默认驱动程序运行得非常缓慢。\n确保将你的显卡驱动程序更新为最新可用版本。在Linux上，你应确保启用了DRI。\n\n除了这些建议之外，你还可以尝试查看调整某些选项是否可以提高性能。\n有关详细信息，请参阅docs/tiles_help.txt。如果做了这一切还是太卡，\n则可以尝试使用非图形的控制台版本，或者等待不依赖OpenGL的未来版本。","question":"贴图版真的好慢！怎样才能让它跑得快点？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: Crawl renders its graphics using OpenGL and SDL. Because it uses OpenGL, it\ndepends on a little bit of hardware acceleration to not run slowly. If your\nsystem is not set up to enable this, then Crawl will run quite poorly.\n\nOn Windows, the default driver that ships with Windows is extremely slow. Make\nsure to update your graphics card driver to the latest one available. On Linux,\nyou should be sure to enable DRI.\n\nIn addition to these suggestions, you can also try to see whether tweaking some\noptions will improve performance. See docs/tiles_help.txt for details. If\nthings are still too laggy after all of that, you could try the non-graphical\nconsole version, or wait for a future version that does not depend on OpenGL.","question":"The Tiles version is awfully slow!
